Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		Call BaglantiliMPSleriDegistir()
	Next
End Sub
Sub BaglantiliMPSleriDegistir()
	Set ss = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			BaglantiliMPS = GetBaglantiliMPS(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), MMPS10T.TRNUM(i))
			if BaglantiliMPS(0) <> "" Then
				if not Doc1Yaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
					Set M10TM = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
					Doc1.Load_Voucher BaglantiliMPS(0)
					Degisti = False
					For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
						if M10TM.TRNUM(j) = BaglantiliMPS(1) Then
							M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j) = MMPS10T.TRNUM(i)
							Degisti = True
							Exit For
						End if
					Next
					if Degisti Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetBaglantiliMPS(MPSNo, YariMamulKodu, MPSTRNUM)
	Dim BaglantiArr(1) 
	QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP'&KAYNAKMPS='" & MPSNo & "'&MAMULSTOKKODU='" & YariMamulKodu & _
				"'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM<>'" & MPSTRNUM & "'"
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dd4gy7jjj7", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		BaglantiArr(0) = M10TM.EVRAKNO
		BaglantiArr(1) = M10TM.TRNUM
	End if
	GetBaglantiliMPS = BaglantiArr
End Function

Function GetBakirMiktari()
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2("dede344", "STOK20T", "KARSITRNUM='" & STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM & "'", "", -1)
	S20T.GroupBy("KOD")

	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", S20T.KOD,1,S20T.SF_MIKTAR,"","","","","","","","") 		
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Doc.d7MsgBox STOK2AT.KOD(i) & " ürün aðacý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
	For j = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "H" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j) = STOK20TI.KOD Then
			GetBakirMiktari = Doc.Round(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j), 3)
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BANKA
Hangi Tablo için : BNK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Banka EFT kodlarýný Aktar (xml)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set objXMLDoc = Nothing
	Set objXMLDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	objXMLDoc.async = False

	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Merkez Bankasý 'na baðlanýlýyor..."
	objXMLDoc.Load "http://eft.tcmb.gov.tr/bankasubelistesi/cif.xml"
'	objXMLDoc.Load "c:\cif.xml"
'	Set x = objXMLDoc.createProcessingInstruction("xml", "version=""1.0"" encoding=""ISO-8859-9""")
'	objXMLDoc.insertBefore x, objXMLDoc.childNodes(0)
'	msgbox objXMLDoc.xml

	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	If Root Is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Merkez Bankasý 'na baðlanýlamadý", "Hata", "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir."
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Set RecList = Root.getElementsByTagName("record")

	For Each x In RecList
		BankaKodu = GetNodeValue(x, "BKOD")
		BankaKodu = Right("0000" & BankaKodu, 4)

		SubeKodu = GetNodeValue(x, "SKOD")
		SubeKodu = Right("00000" & SubeKodu, 5)

		SubeAdi = GetNodeValue(x, "NAME")
		SehirKodu = GetNodeValue(x, "CITY")
		if trim(SubeKodu) = "00000" Then BankaAdi = SubeAdi

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Banka/Þube EFT kodlarý aktarýlýyor : " & BankaKodu & " / " & SubeKodu & " : " & SubeAdi

		if BankaKodu <> EskiBankaKodu Then
			if trim(BNK00.KOD) <> "" Then
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
			Doc.Load_Voucher BankaKodu
			BNK00.AD = BankaAdi
			BNK00T.Empty
		End if
		if trim(SubeKodu) <> "00000" Then
			satirNo = BNK00T.AddRow()
			BNK00T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			BNK00T.SUBE_KODU = SubeKodu
			BNK00T.SUBE_ADI = SubeAdi
			BNK00T.TELEFON2 = SehirKodu
		End if
		EskiBankaKodu = BankaKodu
	Next
	if trim(BNK00.KOD) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetNodeValue(NodeName, AlanAdi)
	Set NodeXml = NodeName.SelectSingleNode(AlanAdi)
	If Not NodeXml Is Nothing Then
		If Not NodeXml.FirstChild Is Nothing Then
			GetNodeValue = NodeXml.FirstChild.Data
		End If
	End If
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : CALC_BOM

Sub Makro1()
	Dim kodArray(3,1)
	KodArray(0,0) = "11"
	KodArray(0,1) = "140099"
	KodArray(1,0) = "15"
	KodArray(1,1) = "16"
	KodArray(2,0) = "200005"
	KodArray(2,1) = "21"
	KodArray(3,0) = "22"
	KodArray(3,1) = "34"

'	Set d = Calc_Bom3("E105 01 007", 100, KodArray)	
'	Set d = Calc_Bom3("E105 01 013", 100, "11..140099;15..16;200005..21;22..34")	
	Set d = GetHmdTable_GK9("E105 01 013", 100, "11..140099;15..16;200005..21;22..34")

	
	Call SendTableToExcel(D)
End Sub
Function GetHmdTable_GK9(StokKodu, Miktar, StopHmdStr)
	Set BomTable = Calc_Bom3(StokKodu, Miktar, StopHmdStr)
	TableStr = BomTable.GetAppName()

	Set HmdTable = Doc.Select2("de3ft5", TableStr, "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to HmdTable.GetRowCount() 
		GK_9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_9", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
		HmdTable.ACIKLAMA(i) = GK_9
		HmdTable.ACIKLAMA2(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
	Next
	HmdTable.GroupBy("ACIKLAMA+ACIKLAMA2")
	Set GetHmdTable_GK9 = HmdTable
End Function
Function Calc_Bom3(StokKodu, Miktar, KodStr)
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		

	KodArr1 = Split(KodStr, ";")
	ub = UBound(KodArr1)
	Dim KodArray()
	ReDim KodArray(ub, 1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(KodArr1)
		KodArr11 = Split(KodArr1(i), "..")
		KodArray(i, 0) = KodArr11(0)
		KodArray(i, 1) = KodArr11(1)
	Next
	Seviye = 100
	satirNo = 1
	Silinecek = False
	i = 0
	While i < Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		i = i + 1
		if Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(i) = 1 Then
			AltSeviyeleriSilinecek = SilinecekMi(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), KodArray)
			if AltSeviyeleriSilinecek Then
				Call AltSeviyeleriSil(Tbmrsys,i)
			Else
				Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
				Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
			End if
		End if
	Wend
	Tbmrsys.SafePack()
	Set Calc_Bom3 = Tbmrsys
End Function
Sub AltSeviyeleriSil(Tbmrsys, ByRef i)
	if i = Tbmrsys.GetRowCount() Then Exit Sub
	seviye = Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(i)
	Do While i < Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		i = i + 1
		if Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(i) > Seviye Then
			Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
			Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
		Else
			Exit Do
		End if
	Loop
	i = i - 1
End Sub
Function SilinecekMi(HammaddeKodu, KodArray)
	SilinecekMi = False
	For i = 0 to UBound(KodArray, 1)
		StopStokKodu1 = KodArray(i,0)
		StopStokKodu2 = KodArray(i,1)
		
		if HammaddeKodu >= StopStokKodu1 and HammaddeKodu <= StopStokKodu2 Then
			SilinecekMi = True
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Calc_KG(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Calc_KG = 0
	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1,Abs(Miktar),"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if UCase(trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), ""))) = "KG" Then
				Calc_KG = Calc_KG + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Calc_KG = Calc_KG * Doc.Div(Miktar, Abs(Miktar))
End Function
Function Calc_BakirKG(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Calc_BakirKG = 0
	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1,Abs(Miktar),"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >= "1" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) < "2" Then
				Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
				if UCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
					kgMiktar = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				Else
					kgMiktar = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Birim, "Kg", d7c_ERRMSG_NO) * Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				End if

				Calc_BakirKG = Calc_BakirKG + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Calc_BakirKG = Calc_BakirKG * Doc.Div(Miktar, Abs(Miktar))
End Function
Function Calc_BakirAlKG(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Dim Arr1(1)
	Arr1(0) = 0
	Arr1(1) = 0
	Calc_BakirAlKG = Arr1

	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1,Abs(Miktar),"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >= "1" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) < "2" Then
				Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
				if UCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
					kgMiktar = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				Else
					kgMiktar = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Birim, "Kg", d7c_ERRMSG_NO) * Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				End if
				if kgMiktar = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox trim(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)) & " stok kartýnda " & trim(Birim) & "->Kg dönüþümü girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
				End if
				Arr1(0) = Arr1(0) + kgMiktar
			Elseif Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >= "2" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <= "22" Then
				Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
				if UCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
					kgMiktar = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				Else
					kgMiktar = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Birim, "Kg", d7c_ERRMSG_NO) * Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				End if
				if kgMiktar = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox trim(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)) & " stok kartýnda " & trim(Birim) & "->Kg dönüþümü girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
				End if
				Arr1(1) = Arr1(1) + kgMiktar
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Arr1(0) = Arr1(0) * Doc.Div(Miktar, Abs(Miktar))
	Arr1(1) = Arr1(1) * Doc.Div(Miktar, Abs(Miktar))

	Calc_BakirAlKG = Arr1 
End Function
Function Calc_KG2(StokKodu, Miktar, KodStr)
	Calc_KG2 = 0
	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	Set Tbmrsys = Calc_Bom3(StokKodu, Abs(Miktar), KodStr)
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if UCase(trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), ""))) = "KG" Then
				Calc_KG2 = Calc_KG2 + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Calc_KG2 = Calc_KG2 * Doc.Div(Miktar, Abs(Miktar))
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : CEKILIST
Hangi Tablo için : STOK19
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çeki Listesi Oluþtur
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()
	Set S19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")
	if trim(S19E.CHKODU) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Cari Hesap Kodu Seçilmemiþ", "Hata", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "PALETNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK67E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLESP=STOK40T(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CHSTOKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MLZ_LIST_BAGLANAN","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KALAN","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

End Sub
Sub SiparisleriGetir()

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESP")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	if trim(MTable.PALETNO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Palet No Boþ", "Hata", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if PaletDahaOnceKullanilmis(MTable.PALETNO) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu palet daha önce kullanýlmýþ.", "Hata", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S67T = Doc.Select1("sw3dde3", "STOK67T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.PALETNO & "'", "", -1)
	if S67T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	S67T.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTable.KOD = S67T.KOD
	MTable.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S67T.KOD, "")
	MTable.SF_MIKTAR = S67T.SF_MIKTAR
	MTable.SF_SF_UNIT = S67T.SF_SF_UNIT

	MTableT.Empty
	Call SipGetir()

	if MTableT.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
		MTableT.SELECTED = "E"
		MTableT.Refresh
	End if
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub SipGetir()
	Set AcikSipTable = GetAcikSip()
	'Set SevkEmriTable = GetSevkEmriTable()

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESP")	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Alanlar = Array("ACIKKAPALI", "EVRAKNO", "ARTNO", "TARIH", "MUSTERIKODU", "KOD", _
					"SF_MIKTAR", "SF_SF_UNIT", "NOTES", "RTESTARIH", "REV_RTESTARIH", "TOLERANSTYPE", _
					"TOLERANS", "SF_PMIKTAR", "SF_IMIKTAR", "SF_NETKAPANANMIK", "CHSIPNO")

	For i = 1 to AcikSipTable.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For each alan in Alanlar
			Deger = AcikSipTable.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, Deger
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.KOD, "")
		MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo), "")
		MTableT.CHSTOKKODU = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(MTable.KOD, MTableT.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo))
		MTableT.MLZ_LIST_BAGLANAN = GetMalzemeListBagliMik(AcikSipTable.ARTNO(i))
		MTableT.KALAN = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR - MTableT.MLZ_LIST_BAGLANAN
	Next

'	For i = 1 to SevkEmriTable.GetRowCount()
'		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
'		For each alan in Alanlar
'			Deger = SevkEmriTable.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
'			MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, Deger
'		Next
'		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(satirNo), "")
'		MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo), "")
'	Next
End Sub
Function GetAcikSip()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set S19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")
	Set GetAcikSip = Doc.Select1("se324443s", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' '&MUSTERIKODU='" & S19E.CHKODU & "'&KOD='" & MTable.KOD & "'", "", -1)
End Function
Function GetSevkEmriTable()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set S19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("de3333", "STOK01", "KOD='" & MTable.KOD & "'&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='SV'", "", -1)
	S01.GroupBy("ALLOC_SIPARTNO")

	Doc.CreateTableObject "de33sssa25r=STOK40T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set GetSevkEmriTable = Doc.GetTableObject("de33sssa25r")
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i), "ARTNO") = S19E.CHKODU Then
			Set S40T = Doc.Select1("dse3c5f5", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S40T.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)

			GetSevkEmriTable.AppendTable(S40T)
		End if
	Next
End Function
Sub CekiListesineAktar()
	Set S19T = Doc.GetTableobject("STOK19T")
	S19T.SafePack()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESP")	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(MTableT.SELECTED(i)) Then
			Set S67T = Doc.Select1("dde3d3d4fg6", "STOK67T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.PALETNO & "'", "", -1)
			For j = 1 to S67T.GetRowCount()
				satirNo =	S19T.AddRow()
				S19T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				S19T.KOD = S67T.KOD(j)
				S19T.LOTNUMBER = S67T.LOTNUMBER(j)
				S19T.SERINO = S67T.SERINO(j)
				S19T.SF_MIKTAR = S67T.SF_MIKTAR(j)
				S19T.SF_SF_UNIT = S67T.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
				S19T.LOCATION1 = S67T.LOCATION1(j)
				S19T.LOCATION2 = S67T.LOCATION2(j)
				S19T.LOCATION3 = S67T.LOCATION3(j)
				S19T.LOCATION4 = S67T.LOCATION4(j)
				S19T.FOKOD = S67T.FOKOD(j)
				S19T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S67T.SF_VRI_VR_C(j)
				S19T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S67T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)
				S19T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S67T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(j)
				S19T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S67T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(j)
				S19T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S67T.SF_VRI_NUM1(j)
				S19T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S67T.SF_VRI_NUM2(j)
				S19T.ALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)
				S19T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ARTNO(i)
				S19T.PACKNO_1 = trim(MTable.PALETNO)
			Next
		End if
	Next

	MTable.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Function PaletDahaOnceKullanilmis(PaletNo)
	PaletDahaOnceKullanilmis = False
	Set S19T = Doc.Select2("de333dedett", "STOK19T", "PACKNO_1='" & trim(PaletNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if S19T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		PaletDahaOnceKullanilmis = True
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set S19T = Doc.Select1("de333dedett", "STOK19T", "PACKNO_1='" & trim(PaletNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if S19T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		PaletDahaOnceKullanilmis = True
	End if
End Function
Function GetMalzemeListBagliMik(SipArtNo)
	Set S19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")
	GetMalzemeListBagliMik = 0
	Set S19T = Doc.Select1("de3ff5gt6", "STOK19T", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & SipArtNo & "'&EVRAKNO<>'" & S19E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		GetMalzemeListBagliMik = GetMalzemeListBagliMik + S19T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next

	Set S19T = Doc.Select2("de3ff5gt6", "STOK19T", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & SipArtNo & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount()
		GetMalzemeListBagliMik = GetMalzemeListBagliMik + S19T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to STOK20TI.GetRowCount()
		if Left(STOK20TI.KOD(i), 3) = "150" Then
			STOK20TI.KOD(i) = "100000"
		End if
	Next
	Doc.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("d4f566yhh6", "MMPS10TM", "KAYNAKDAGITIMANAHTAR=0", "", -1)
	M10TM.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
			MMPS10TM.KAYNAKDAGITIMANAHTAR(j) = 1
		Next
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Makro Kodu : DT-MALIYET

Sub Makro1()
	for i = 1 to STOK24T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Maliyetler hesaplanýyor " & i & " / " & STOK24T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK24T.KOD(i)) <> "" then
			Redim A(7)
			A(0)=STOK24T.KOD(i)
			Tarih = STOK24E.TARIH
			If Tarih < "2005/05/04" Then Tarih = "2005/05/04"
'			Maliyet = Doc.StandartMaliyet(A,1,STOK24T.SF_SF_UNIT(i),Tarih,"TK","TK",Doc.DefaultPB)
'			Maliyet = Doc.SonHesaplanMaliyet(STOK24T.KOD(i),STOK24E.AMBCODE,STOK24E.TARIH)
			Maliyet = Doc.SonHesaplanMaliyet(STOK24T.KOD(i),"",STOK24E.TARIH)
			if Maliyet = 0 Then 
				Maliyet = GetSonrakiMaliyet(STOK24T.KOD(i), STOK24E.TARIH)
			End if
			STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			STOK24T.OR_FIYAT(i) = Doc.Round(Maliyet,6)
			STOK24T.PUNIT_ORG(i) = Doc.DefaultPB
		end if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetSonrakiMaliyet(StokKodu, Tarih)
	GetSonrakiMaliyet = 0
	Set S10A = Doc.Select1("s33", "STOK10A", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&TARIH>='" & Tarih & "'&TL_TUTAR>0", "TARIH", 1)
	if S10A.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonrakiMaliyet = Doc.Div(S10A.TL_TUTAR + S10A.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR, S10A.SF_MIKTAR)
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "STOK29T")
	Call CreateTableStr(Grid)
End Sub
Sub CreateTableStr(Grid)
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
'		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
'		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
		Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			Str1 = "MTable.Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""DATE"",10,0,""L"","""","""""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			Str1 = "MTable.Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""CHAR""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""L"","""",Grupkodu"
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			Str1 = "MTable.Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""CHAR""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""L"",""F2=DO_BRW('"" & Tablo & ""')"","""""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
			Str1 = "MTable.Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""CHAR""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""L"","""","""""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			Str1 = "MTable.Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""DOUBLE""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""R"","""","""""
		End if
		TableStr = TableStr & Chr(9) & Str1 & vbcrlf
	Next
	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", TableStr)
End Sub
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Etiket Kartlarýný Yarat - Silmeyin !!!!!!!!
Kod : 
Function GetEtiketTable(EtiketFormu)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK20_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK68_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SAYM01" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SAYM01_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK60_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOKFR2" or Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK29_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK36_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK19_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK26T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
 		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK61_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK24_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK46" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK46_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SFDC32" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SFDC32_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, Miktar)
	End if
End Function
Function MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	M10TM.SafePack()
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Miktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j)
		'DefKutuIciMiktar = GetStdKoliAdet(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j), M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j), "")
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "KOLI_MIK",0)
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)

		'EtkTarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETK_TRH",0)

		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = DefKutuIciMiktar
		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETIK_ADT",0)	
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then EtiketAdedi = 1

		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			Redim IDArray(1,0)
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			SeriNo = trim(M10E.EVRAKNO)
			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "")
			d7LB.SERINO = SeriNo
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
				CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
			End if
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = ""
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10E.EVRAKNO

			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Next
	Next

	Set MMPS10_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set S25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	S25T.SafePack()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		SeriNo = S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SeriNo), 30)
		Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if trim(Barkod) <> "" and D7LB_111.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher SeriNo
	
			d7LB.SKOD = S25T.NEWCODE(i)
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK25"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)

			OncekiBarkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.SERINO(i)), 30)
			Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiBarkod & "'", "", -1)

			if D7LB_Eski.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = OncekiBarkod
				d7LB.ILKETIKET = OncekiBarkod
				d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = D7LB_Eski.CH_TEDARIKCI
				d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = D7LB_Eski.SIPARISNO_46
				d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = D7LB_Eski.SIPARTNO_46
				d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = D7LB_Eski.CH_MUSTERI
				d7LB.FORMKODU = D7LB_Eski.FORMKODU
				d7LB.STATU = D7LB_Eski.STATU
			Else
				FG_Array = GetFasonInfo(S25T.KOD(i), S25T.LOTNUMBER(i), S25T.SERINO(i))
				d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = FG_Array(0)
				d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = FG_Array(1)
				d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = FG_Array(2)
				d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			End if
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25E.TARGETAMBCODE
			
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Else
			d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_111)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set STOK25_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function

			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = MTableT2.SF_SF_UNIT(i)

			d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = MTableT2.FOKOD(i)

			d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S29E.AMBCODE
			d7LB.LOCATION1 = MTableT2.LOCATION1(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION2 = MTableT2.LOCATION2(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION3 = MTableT2.LOCATION3(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION4 = MTableT2.LOCATION4(i)
			d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = S29E.CARIHESAPCODE
			d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = MTableT2.SIPNO(i)
			d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = MTableT2.SIPARTNO(i)
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S29E.TARIH
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Else
			d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_111)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set STOK29_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function

Function  SFDC32_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, Miktar)
	Set S321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	Set WS = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	Barkod = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
	d7LB.SKOD = S321E.STOK_CODE
	d7LB.APPKODU = "SFDC32"
	d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "S4"
'	d7LB.LOTNUMBER = MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
	d7LB.SERINO = S321E.MPSNO
	d7LB.MPSNO = S321E.MPSNO
	d7LB.REFTEXT501 = S321E.JOBNO
	d7LB.REFTEXT502 = GetSonrakiOp(S321E.MPSNO, S321E.JOBNO)
	d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S321E.X_T_ISMERKEZI
	d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S321E.OPERASYON
	d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = WS.OPERATOR

	d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
'	d7LB.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_SF_UNIT"(i)
	d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MUSTERIKODU", S321E.MPSNO, "")
	d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
	d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun

	Doc1.Save_Voucher

	Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
	d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set SFDC32_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function 
Function SAYM01_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)

			d7LB.SERINO = MTableT2.SERINO(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = MTableT2.SF_SF_UNIT(i)

			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)

			d7LB.LOCATION2 = MTableT2.LOCATION2(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION3 = MTableT2.LOCATION3(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION4 = MTableT2.LOCATION4(i)
			d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = ""
			d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = ""
			d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = ""
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu

		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set SAYM01_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function GetFasonInfo(Kod, LotNo, SeriNo)
	Dim FasonArr(2)
	QStr = "KOD='" & Kod & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & LotNo & "'&SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1("deefft66", "STOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		FasonArr(0) = S68T.CHCODE
		FasonArr(1) = S68T.SIPNO
		FasonArr(2) = S68T.SIPARTNO
	End if
	GetFasonInfo = FasonArr
End Function
Function GetSonrakiOp(MPSNo, IsNo)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("ded44hh7j", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonrakiOp = M10T.JOBNO
	Else
		GetSonrakiOp = "DEPO"
	End if
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET1
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Etiket Yarat
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	'MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	'MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=MMPS10T(NOFILE)","IO")
	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STACK","IO")
	Call AddMTable2Fields(MTableT2)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	Call OperasyonlariOku()

End Sub
Sub OperasyonlariOku()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("dede333", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	MTableT.Empty()

	Alanlar = M10T.GetFieldNames("")

	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		For each alan in Alanlar
			alanDegeri = M10T.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketYarat()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	IsNo = MTableT.JOBNO
	if trim(IsNo) = "" Then
		Doc.d7Msgbox "Üstteki 'Operasyonlar' bölümünden istenen operasyonu seçtikten sonra tekrar deneyiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	
	EtiketSayisi = InputBox(trim(MTableT.R_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD) & " operasyonu için kaç adet etiket basýlacak")
	EtiketSayisi = Doc.CDbl(EtiketSayisi)
	if EtiketSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "MPSNO='" & M10E.EVRAKNO & "'&REFTEXT501='" & trim(IsNo) & "'"
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("se33332ss", "D7KIDSLB", QStr, "<KOD", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SonNumara = 0
	Else
		SonNumara = Doc.CDbl(Right(trim(D7LB.KOD), 4))
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	For i = 1 to EtiketSayisi
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiketler yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & EtiketSayisi
		Call EtiketKartiYarat(SonNumara + i, Doc1)
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	Call EtiketleriGoster()
End Sub
Sub EtiketKartiYarat(EtiketNo, Doc1)
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	
	OpSiraNo = trim(MTableT.R_SIRANO)
	EtiketKartNo = trim(M10E.EVRAKNO) & "-" & OpSiraNo & "-" & Right("0000" & EtiketNo, 4)

	Doc1.Load_Voucher EtiketKartNo
	
	d7LB.SKOD = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU	
	d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
	d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM
	d7LB.LOTNUMBER = trim(M10E.EVRAKNO) & "-" & OpSiraNo & "-" & EtiketNo
	d7LB.REFTEXT501 = trim(MTableT.JOBNO)

	Doc1.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketleriGoster()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	IsNo = MTableT.JOBNO
	if trim(IsNo) = "" Then
		Doc.d7Msgbox "Üstteki 'Operasyonlar' bölümünden istenen operasyonu seçtikten sonra tekrar deneyiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	MTableT2.Empty

	QStr = "MPSNO='" & M10E.EVRAKNO & "'&REFTEXT501='" & trim(IsNo) & "'"
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("se33332ss", "D7KIDSLB", QStr, "", -1)

	Alanlar = MTableT2.GetFieldNames("")

	For i = 1 to D7LB.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		For each alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = D7LB.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
		MTableT2.SKOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.SKOD(satirNo), "")
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddMTable2Fields(MTable2)
	MTable2.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR", 30, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW('D7KIDSLB')", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SKOD", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "F2=DO_KBRWD('STOK00')", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SKOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER", "CHAR", 12, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "FOKOD", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW('FOSYSFT')", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "LOCATION1", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", "LOCAT1"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "LOCATION2", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", "LOCAT2"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "LOCATION3", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", "LOCAT3"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "LOCATION4", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", "LOCAT4"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", "STUNIT"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 16, MIK, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "STOK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 16, MIK, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR", 20, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_RECETE", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_VR_C", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", "SCVAR1"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_VR_R1", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_VR_R2", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_VR_R3", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_NUM1", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "VRI_NUM2", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""

Sub EtiketYazdir()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Yarimamul Tanitim Karti" & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	EtkTuru = InputBox("Etiket türünü seçiniz:" & vbcrlf & "1. Yarýmamul talimat" & vbcrlf & "2. Ýhracat etiketi" & _
			vbcrlf & "3. Granosan etiketi", "Etiket türü", "1")

	if EtkTuru <> "2" Then
		For i = 1 to 2
			siraNo = siraNo  + 1
			if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
			KKEtiket(pe1)
		Next
	End if

	AktifSatir = MTableT2.GetCurrentRow()
	For i = AktifSatir to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
'	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		if EtkTuru <> "3" Then
			siraNo = siraNo  + 1
			if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call IzlenebilirlikEtiketiYazdir(pe1, i)
		End if
		if EtkTuru <> "2" Then
			siraNo = siraNo  + 1
			if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, i)
		End if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub IzlenebilirlikEtiketiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	Gen1 = 16
	Gen2 = 16
	Gen3 = 14
	Gen4 = 24
	Gen5 = 17

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ýþ Emri No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Operasyon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen3, "Ýþ Merkezi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen5, "Operatör")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, solMargin + 1, 6, Gen1, trim(MTableT2.MPSNO(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 7, Gen2, Trim(MTableT.R_OPERASYON))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 8, Gen3, Trim(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, trim(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU))
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop - 1, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen5)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin + 1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Taným : " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, solMargin + 2, 5, 26, "Hazýrlýk")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 35, 5, 26, "Üretim")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 66, 10, 26, "Üretilen" & vbcrlf & "Miktar")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3, solMargin + 2, 5, 11.5, "Baslama")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 5, 11.5, "Bitis")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 35, 5, 11.5, "Baslama")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 5, 11.5, "Bitis")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop + 3, solMargin + 2, 4.5, 11.5)
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 4.5, 11.5)

	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 35, 4.5, 11.5)
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 4.5, 11.5)

	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 66, 4.5, 26)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 7)

	Gen11 = 14
	Gen12 = 46
	Gen13 = 16
	Gen14 = 13

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen11, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen12, "Taným")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen13, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen14, "Fiili Kullaným")

	Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("d33cdgg", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & MTableT.R_SIRANO & "'", "", -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	satirPozr = pe1.GetTop
	OncekiOp = GetOncekiOp()
	if trim(OncekiOp) <> "" Then
		satirPozr = satirPozr + 4
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen11, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", OncekiOp, ""))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen12, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 3, Gen13 - 1)
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 3, Gen14)			
	End if

	For i = 1 to M10TH.GetRowCount()
		satirPozr = satirPozr + 4
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen11, M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen12, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), ""))
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 3, Gen13 - 1)
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 3, Gen14)			
	Next

	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 13, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 10, "Lot No :")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1.5, solMargin + 1, 12, 40, MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop - 1, solMargin + 40, 10, 50, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
End Sub
Sub YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 11, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 10,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 0.5, solMargin + 35, 15, sagMargin - solMargin - 40, "YARIMAMUL TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 13
	Aralik = 4.5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Taným")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 11)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ýþ Merk.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 15, ": " & trim(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 10, "Opr. :")
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4.5, 22)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, 8, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	YaziAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, solMargin + 5, 12, 50, MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin + 3, 10, 50, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(YaziAlt + 2, solMargin + 58, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 58)
	Ust = pe1.GetTop
	sol = pe1.GetLeft
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 0.3, sol, 5, sagMargin - sol, "Kalite Kontrol")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub KKEtiket(pe1)
	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3


	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	if MTableT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 8.5, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 0.5, solMargin, 6, sagMargin - solMargin, "YARIMAMUL TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 13
	Aralik = 4.5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 11, solMargin + 1, 5, 8, "Tarih:")
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4.5, 12)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 6, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 20, ": " & M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 7, "Taným")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, 10, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 18, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 12, "Ýþ Merk.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 15, ": " & trim(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 10, "Opr. :")
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4.5, 22)

	Set TECH10 = GetMuhendislik()
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, 17, "Talimatlar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Basla = pe1.GetTop
	Aralik1 = 3.5
	For i = 1 To TECH10.GetRowCount
		if i < TECH10.GetRowCount Then
			if Left(TECH10.NOTES(i+1), 2) = ".." Then
				Tanim = trim(TECH10.NOTES(i)) & " " & Mid(TECH10.NOTES(i+1), 3)
			Else
				Tanim = TECH10.NOTES(i)
			End if
		Else
			Tanim = TECH10.NOTES(i)
		End if

		if Left(TECH10.NOTES(i), 2) <> ".." Then
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
			if i = 1 Then
				satirPozr = Basla + Aralik1
			Else
				satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom + 0.5
			End if
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 1, -2, sagMargin - Solmargin - 1,siraNo & ". " & Tanim)
		End if
	Next

	YaziAlt = AltMargin - 17.5

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 12, solMargin + 5, 12, 50, trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(1)) & " KK")
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 5, solMargin + 3, 5.5, 50, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(1)) & " KK", "")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(YaziAlt + 2, solMargin + 58, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 58)
	Ust = pe1.GetTop
	sol = pe1.GetLeft
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 0.3, sol, 5, sagMargin - sol, "Kalite Kontrol")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
Function GetMuhendislik()
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	SiraNo = M10T.R_SIRANO
	QStr = "TABLE1='MMPS10E'&KRITER='" & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO) & "'&KOD='" & trim(SiraNo) & "'"
	Set GetMuhendislik = Doc.Select1("sw2edccc", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1)
End Function
Function GetOncekiOp()
	GetOncekiOp = ""
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	if MTableT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	IsNo = MTableT2.REFTEXT501
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MTableT.JOBNO(i)) = trim(IsNo) Then Exit Function
		GetOncekiOp = MTableT.R_OPERASYON(i)
	Next
End Function
Sub KangalEtiketi()
	sayfaBoyu	= 60
	sayfaEni = 80
	UstMargin = 2
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 8
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Kangal Etiketi" & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	Call KangalEtiketiYazdir(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub KangalEtiketiYazdir(pe1)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
' 	Logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 12, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 11,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

'	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 10, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 10, "ALTIN KABLO")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, solMargin + 10, 6, sagMargin - solMargin - 10, "SANAYÝÝ ANONÝM ÞÝRKETÝ")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 20
	Gen2 = 17
	Aralik = 3.7
	Aralik2 = 10
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12.5, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ürün Kodu")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	Kesit = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "KESIT", 0)
	Cinsi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "CINSI", 0)
	Gerilim = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "GERILIM", 0)
	Renk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "RENK", 0)
	Standard = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "STANDART", 0)
	Boy = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "BOY", 0)
	Boy = Doc.NumFormat(Boy, 15, Doc.SigDig(Boy))

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Kesit(mm2)")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, Kesit)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Cinsi")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 1, Cinsi)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Standard")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, Standard)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Gerilim")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, Gerilim)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Renk")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 1, Renk)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER))

	AltCizgiLen = 8
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - AltCizgiLen, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 45 , AltCizgiLen, 0)
'	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(AltMargin - AltCizgiLen + 1, solMargin + 3, AltCizgiLen - 2, 38, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER), "")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 7, solMargin + 42, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 44, Boy & " m")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKETSIL
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to STOK20TI.GetRowCount()
		Kod = STOK20TI.KOD(i)
		if Left(Kod, 1) = "I" or Left(KOD, 1) = "M" Then
			STOK20TI.KOD(i) = ""
			STOK20TI.SF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	STOK20TI.SafePack()
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-GOSTER
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call EtiketleriGoster()
End Sub
Sub EtiketleriGoster()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	IsNo = MTableT.JOBNO
	if trim(IsNo) = "" Then
		Doc.d7Msgbox "Üstteki 'Operasyonlar' bölümünden istenen operasyonu seçtikten sonra tekrar deneyiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	MTableT2.Empty

	QStr = "MPSNO='" & M10E.EVRAKNO & "'&REFTEXT501='" & trim(IsNo) & "'"
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("se33332ss", "D7KIDSLB", QStr, "", -1)

	Alanlar = MTableT2.GetFieldNames("")

	For i = 1 to D7LB.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		For each alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = D7LB.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
		MTableT2.SKOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.SKOD(satirNo), "")
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-KNG
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()
	Call KangalEtiketi()
End Sub
Sub KangalEtiketi()
	sayfaBoyu	= 60
	sayfaEni = 80
	UstMargin = 2
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 8
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Kangal Etiketi" & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = siraNo  + 1
		if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call KangalEtiketiYazdir(pe1, i)
		Exit For
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub KangalEtiketiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
' 	Logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 12, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 11,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

'	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 10, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 10, "ALTIN KABLO")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, solMargin + 10, 6, sagMargin - solMargin - 10, "SANAYÝÝ ANONÝM ÞÝRKETÝ")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 20
	Gen2 = 17
	Aralik = 3.7
	Aralik2 = 10
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12.5, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ürün Kodu")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	Kesit = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "KESIT", 0)
	Cinsi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "CINSI", 0)
	Gerilim = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "GERILIM", 0)
	Renk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "RENK", 0)
	Standard = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "STANDART", 0)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Kesit(mm2)")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, Kesit)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Cinsi")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 1, Cinsi)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Standard")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, Standard)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Gerilim")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, Gerilim)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Renk")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 1, Renk)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst + Aralik2, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	satirUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen1, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirUst, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen2, MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))

	AltCizgiLen = 8
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - AltCizgiLen, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 45 , AltCizgiLen, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(AltMargin - AltCizgiLen + 1, solMargin + 3, AltCizgiLen - 2, 38, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 7, solMargin + 42, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 44, "100 m")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-YARAT
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin

Sub Makro1()
	Call EtiketYazdir()
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Yarimamul Tanitim Karti" & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = siraNo  + 1
		if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
		if not (M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU > "22" and M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU < "34") Then
			Call IzlenebilirlikEtiketiYazdir(pe1, i)
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		End if
		Call YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, i)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub IzlenebilirlikEtiketiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	Gen1 = 16
	Gen2 = 16
	Gen3 = 14
	Gen4 = 24
	Gen5 = 17

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ýþ Emri No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Operasyon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen3, "Ýþ Merkezi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen5, "Operatör")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, solMargin + 1, 6, Gen1, trim(MTableT2.MPSNO(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 7, Gen2, Trim(MTableT.R_OPERASYON))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 8, Gen3, Trim(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, trim(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU))
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop - 1, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen5)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin + 1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Taným : " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, solMargin + 2, 5, 26, "Hazýrlýk")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 35, 5, 26, "Üretim")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 66, 10, 26, "Üretilen" & vbcrlf & "Miktar")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3, solMargin + 2, 5, 11.5, "Baslama")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 5, 11.5, "Bitis")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 35, 5, 11.5, "Baslama")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 5, 11.5, "Bitis")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop + 3, solMargin + 2, 4.5, 11.5)
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 4.5, 11.5)

	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 35, 4.5, 11.5)
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 3, 4.5, 11.5)

	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 66, 4.5, 26)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 7)

	Gen11 = 14
	Gen12 = 46
	Gen13 = 16
	Gen14 = 13

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen11, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen12, "Taným")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen13, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen14, "Fiili Kullaným")

	Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("d33cdgg", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & MTableT.R_SIRANO & "'", "", -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	satirPozr = pe1.GetTop
	For i = 1 to M10TH.GetRowCount()
		satirPozr = satirPozr + 4
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen11, M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen12, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), ""))
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 3, Gen13 - 1)
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 3, Gen14)			
	Next

	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 13, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 10, "Lot No :")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1.5, solMargin + 1, 12, 40, MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop - 1, solMargin + 40, 10, 50, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
End Sub
Sub YMamulTanitimKartiYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo2.bmp"
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 11, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, solmargin + 1, 10,30, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 0.5, solMargin + 35, 15, sagMargin - solMargin - 40, "YARIMAMUL TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 13
	Aralik = 4.5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Taným")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 11)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ýþ Merk.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 15, ": " & trim(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 10, "Opr. :")
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4.5, 22)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, 8, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	YaziAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, solMargin + 5, 12, 50, MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin + 3, 10, 50, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)), "")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(YaziAlt + 2, solMargin + 58, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 58)
	Ust = pe1.GetTop
	sol = pe1.GetLeft
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 0.3, sol, 5, sagMargin - sol, "Kalite Kontrol")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S

Sub Makro1()
	k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Tüm ürün aðaçlarýndaki Extruder hat hýzlarýný hesaplamak istediðinize emin misiniz?", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)
	if k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	Doc.LoadFirst_Voucher
	Do
		if HizHesapla() > 0 Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Loop While Doc.LoadNext_Voucher = 0 
End Sub
Function HizHesapla()
	HizHesapla = 0
	For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
		if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "I" and _
				(Left(BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i),1) = "K" or Left(BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i),2) = "IZ")  Then
			Hiz = GetHiz(BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO(i))
			if Hiz > 0 Then
				BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(i) 
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BV = Hiz
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU = "mamul/dk"
				HizHesapla = HizHesapla + 1
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetHiz(TezgahKodu, SiraNo)
	GetHiz = 0
	Set B01T = Doc.Select2("def54y66", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_SIRANO='" & SiraNo & "'", "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	Set I00 = Doc.Select1("d4r5555", "IMLT00", "KOD='" & TezgahKodu & "'", "", -1)
	PVCBasmaKapasitesi = I00.B_MASRF_DGTM_KATS4
	MaxCekiciHizi = I00.B_MASRF_DGTM_KATS5
	BazYogunluk = 1.62	

	For i = 1 to B01T.GetRowCount()
'		PlastikYogunluk = GetPlastikYogunluk(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i))
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("de45g66", "STOK00", "KOD='" & B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'", "", -1)
		PlastikYogunluk = S00.B_YOGUNLUK
		if PlastikYogunluk > 0 Then
			YeniBasmaKapasitesi = PVCBasmaKapasitesi * PlastikYogunluk / BazYogunluk 'kg/saat
			GetHiz = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR * YeniBasmaKapasitesi, B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) * 60), 0) 'm/dk
			if GetHiz > MaxCekiciHizi Then GetHiz = MaxCekiciHizi
		End if
	Next
End Function
