----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FIYAT
Hangi Tablo için : STOK70
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ýhracat Eskale Fiyatlarý Hesapla
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set df = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	STOK70T.Safepack()
	For i = 1 to STOK70T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Fiyatlar hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK70T.GetRecCount()
		KapanisLME = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",STOK70T.SIPNO(i),"KAP_LME",0)
		YeniFiyat = EskaleFiyat(STOK70T.S60_SIPARTNO(i), KapanisLME)
		STOK70T.SetCurrentRow(i)
		STOK70T.OR_FIYAT = Doc.Round(YeniFiyat,5)
	Next
	if STOK70T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then STOK70T.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Function EskaleFiyat(SipartNo, KapanisLME)
	SiparisArtikelNo = SipartNo
	SiparisNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T","EVRAKNO", SipartNo,"ARTNO")
	AcilisLME = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",SiparisNo,"ACILIS_LME",0)
	StokKodu =  Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T","KOD", SipartNo,"ARTNO")

	if AcilisLME = 0 or KapanisLME = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox SiparisNo & " no.lu sipariþte Baz ve/veya Kapanýþ LME deðerleri girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
	End if

	BakirAgirligi = GetBakirAgirligi(StokKodu, 1) 'kg/km

	EskalasyonFarki = (KapanisLME - AcilisLME) * BakirAgirligi / 1000 / 1000
	SiparisFiyati = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T","OR_FIYAT",SiparisArtikelNo,"ARTNO")

'	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
'	YuzdeIskonto = IskontoOrani(satirNo)
	YuzdeIskonto = 0
	EskaleFiyat = (SiparisFiyati + EskalasyonFarki) * (1 - YuzdeIskonto / 100) 
End Function
Function EskaleFiyat1(satirNo)
	SiparisArtikelNo = STOK70T.S60_SIPARTNO(satirNo)
	SiparisNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", STOK70T.SIPNO(satirNo),"")
'	AcilisLME = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",SiparisNo,"ACILIS_LME",0)
'	KapanisLME = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",SiparisNo,"KAP_LME",0)
	AcilisLME = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40T",SiparisArtikelNo,"ACILIS_LME",0)
	KapanisLME = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40T",SiparisArtikelNo,"KAP_LME",0)

	if AcilisLME = 0 or KapanisLME = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox SiparisNo & " no.lu sipariþte Baz ve/veya Kapanýþ LME deðerleri girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
	End if

	BakirAgirligi = GetBakirAgirligi(STOK70T.KOD(satirNo), 1)

	EskalasyonFarki = (KapanisLME - AcilisLME) * BakirAgirligi / 1000 / 1000
	SiparisFiyati = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T","OR_FIYAT",SiparisArtikelNo,"ARTNO")

	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	YuzdeIskonto = IskontoOrani(satirNo)
	EskaleFiyat = (SiparisFiyati + EskalasyonFarki) * (1 - YuzdeIskonto / 100) 
End Function
Function IskontoOrani(satirNo)
	IskontoOrani = 0
	Exit Function
	SiparisArtikelNo = STOK70T.S60_SIPARTNO(satirNo)
	SipPaketIcerigi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T","SF_IMIKTAR",SiparisArtikelNo,"ARTNO")
	SevkBoyu = STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	StokKodu = STOK70T.KOD(satirNo)
	GK6 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", StokKodu, "")

	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	if trim(CHKodu) = "000004" then 'CLEVELAND
'		if SipPaketIcerigi < 1000 then 
'			SevkBoyu = SevkBoyu * 1000 / SipPaketIcerigi
'		end if
		if StokKodu >= "ZR" and StokKodu < "ZR 105 015" and trim(GK6) <= "016" Then
			if SevkBoyu > 999 then
				IskontoOrani = 0
			elseif SevkBoyu >= 800 and SevkBoyu <= 999 then
				IskontoOrani = 0
			elseif SevkBoyu >= 600 and SevkBoyu <= 799 then
				IskontoOrani = 0
			elseif SevkBoyu >= 300 and SevkBoyu <= 599 then
				IskontoOrani = 0
			else
				IskontoOrani = 0
			end if
		Elseif StokKodu >= "ZR" and StokKodu < "ZR 105 015" and (trim(GK6) = "025" or trim(GK6) = "035") Then
			if SevkBoyu > 499 then
				IskontoOrani = 0
			elseif SevkBoyu >= 250 and SevkBoyu <= 499 then
				IskontoOrani = 0
			else
				IskontoOrani = 0
			end if
		End if
	else
		IskontoOrani = 0 'nasýl olacak?
		exit Function
	End If
End Function
Function GetBakirAgirligi(StokKodu, Miktar)
	KartAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", StokKodu, "")
	if Doc.Round(KartAgirlik, 3) > 0 Then
		GetBakirAgirligi = KartAgirlik * Miktar
		Exit Function
	End if
	BakirAgirligi = 0
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1, Miktar,"","","","","","","","")
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		Hmd = Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i),3)
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" and (Hmd = "H00" or Hmd = "H01") Then
			BakirAgirligi = BakirAgirligi + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
		End if
	Next
	GetBakirAgirligi = Doc.Round(BakirAgirligi, 3)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Excel.Sheets(1)

	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T_LIST")
	For j = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		if Grid(j,2) <> "..." Then
			Sh1.Cells(1,j+1).Value = Grid(j,2)
		End if
	Next	
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		For j = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
			if Grid(j,2) <> "..." Then
				AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
				if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "COLVT") = 0 Then
					if Grid(j,4) = "N" Then
						Sh1.Cells(i+1, j+1).Value = MMPS10T_LIST.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
						Sh1.Cells(i+1, j+1).Select
						Excel.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0"
					Elseif Grid(j,4) = "D" Then
						Sh1.Cells(i+1, j+1).Value = "'" & Doc.StrToNorDate(MMPS10T_LIST.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i))
					Else
						Sh1.Cells(i+1, j+1).Value = "'" & MMPS10T_LIST.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
					End if
				Else
					AlanAdi = Replace(AlanAdi, "COLVT", "COLVD")
					Sh1.Cells(i+1, j+1).Select
					Excel.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0"
					Sh1.Cells(i+1, j+1).Value = MMPS10T_LIST.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				End if
			End if
		Next
	Next
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1),Sh1.Cells(1,30)).Select 
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	
	Sh1.Rows(1).RowHeight = 53
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True

	For i = 1 to 30
		Sh1.Columns(i).EntireColumn.AutoFit
	Next
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Call HurdaDagitimKalanlariHesapla()
End Sub
Sub HurdaDagitimKalanlariHesapla()
	Set MTableHrd = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEHRD")
	sNo = MTableHrd.GetCurrentRow()

	For i = 1 to MTableHrd.GetRowCount()
		MTableHrd.SetCurrentRow(i)
		Set MT = Doc.Select2("de222222", "MAKROTABLET", "KOD_STOK00_GK_7='" & trim(MTableHrd.KOD) & "'", "", -1)
		if MT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MT.GroupBy2("KOD_STOK00_GK_7")
			MTableHrd.DUSUM_MIKTAR = MT.HURDA_AGIRLIK
		Else
			MTableHrd.DUSUM_MIKTAR = 0
		End if
	Next	
	MTableHrd.SetCurrentRow(sNo)
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KALITE1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kalite
Kod : 
Dim Q10T
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')=",""

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : _GNPRG2
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : MAKROFORMU1
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : Makro1
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 

FORM MAKROFORMU1 CAPTIONTYPE=SYS

	DEFINEFONT ID="default_groupbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
	SET DEFAULTFONT_GROUPBOX=default_groupbox_font

	SET CAPTION="Kalite"

	MENUDEF
		POPUP TEXT="Dosya"
		MENUITEM TEXT="Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" ID=ID_APP_EXIT //KEY=ESCAPE
		ENDPOPUP
	ENDMENUDEF

	
	RTEXT LEFT=10P TOP=6p W=75p T="MPS No "
	EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10p TOP=+0 D="MAKROTABLE.MPSNO" BROWSEBUTTON=1

	RTEXT LEFT=10P TOP=E+3p W=75p T="Stok Kodu"
	EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10p TOP=+0 D="MAKROTABLE.KOD" BROWSEBUTTON=1
	LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D="MAKROTABLE.KOD_STOK00_AD" BORDER=ETCHED BCKCOL=RGB_232_232_232

	RTEXT LEFT=10P TOP=E+3p W=75p T="Lot No"

	BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+5p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=38p CAPTION="Test Sonuçlarý Giriþi" ID="MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KaliteTestSonuclariGirisi')"

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KALITE2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kalite Test Sonuçlarý Giriþi
Kod : 
Dim Q10T
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_KALITE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	
	Set MTableMG = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEMG")
	QVLCODE1 = Doc.Customp_Gets("QVLCODE1")
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "K") > 0 Then
		StokKodu = MTableMG.KOD
	End if
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "L") > 0 Then
		LotNo = MTableMG.LOTNUMBER
	End if
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "S") > 0 Then
		SeriNo = MTableMG.SERINO
	End if

	Set par = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	par.SetParameter "CREATE_QVAL_DEF_TABLE","E" 

	ret = Doc.QVAL01E_CREATEEDITTABLE("___QVAL___",StokKodu, LotNo, SeriNo, par)
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")

'	TblStr = 	par.GetParameter("_QVAL_DEF_TABLE")
'msgbox TblStr
	TblStr = "_QVAL_DEF_TABLE_110234"
	Set Q10T = Doc.GetTableObject(TblStr)

'	Call AlanAdlariniveEventleriEkle(Q10T, KaliteTable)

	MTable.KOD = MTableMG.KOD
	MTable.LOTNUMBER = MTableMG.LOTNUMBER
	MTable.SERINO = MTableMG.SERINO
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "KALITE",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Sub KaliteYaz()
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")
	KaliteTable.Exec "Save"
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Kalite parametreleri kaydedildi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(Q10T, KaliteTable)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
	En = 0
	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1024
		Boy = 690
	Else
		En = En - 25
		Boy = Boy - 60
	End if 
	TabEn = En - 5
	TabBoy = Boy - 80

	GridEn = En - 5
	GridBoy = TabBoy - 65

	FormStr = "FORM KALITE HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Kalite" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET FIXEDPITCH_EDIT=1" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "DEFINEFONT ID=""SABIT"" FACE=""Courier New"" POINT=10 //BOLD=Y " & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "DEFINEFONT ID=""BOLD"" FACE=""MS Sans Serif"" POINT=8 BOLD=Y " & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "GROUPBOX TEXT="""" AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=35 TOP=+0 WIDTH=300p HEIGHT=80p BORDER=BUMP" & vbCrLf
	
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+5p TOP=+10p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Stok Kodu'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.KOD" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Lot No'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.LOTNUMBER" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Seri No'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.SERINO" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGROUPBOX" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCX" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=80 TOP=15p WIDTH=15 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KaliteYaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+5p WIDTH=15 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbcrlf


	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCX" & vbCrLf

	QValStr = GetQValStr(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	FormStr = FormStr & QValStr & vbCrLf
	'FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetQValStr(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	Ust = 4
	IkinciBlok = 11
'	QValStr = "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
 	QValStr = "FRAME AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=1P TOP=E+5p WIDTH=1010p HEIGHT=500P HORIZONTALSCROLLBAR=NO BORDER=0" & vbcrlf
	For i = 1 to Q10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(Q10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = Q10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
'		Aciklama = trim(Q10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		Aciklama = Q10T.VARASPNAME(i)
		AlanTuru = trim(Q10T.VARTYPE(i))
		AlanGenisligi = Q10T.VARLEN(i)
		Grupkodu = trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))

'		if i >= IkinciBlok Then Left1 = 68 Else Left1 = 1
'		if i = IkinciBlok Then QValStr = QValStr & "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
		Left1 = 1
		QValStr = QValStr & "RTEXT LEFT=" & Left1 & " TOP=E+0 T='" & Aciklama & "' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Else
			QValStr = QValStr & "EDITTEXT "
		End if
		QValStr = QValStr & "LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 "
		if AlanGenisligi > 50 Then QValStr = QValStr & "WIDTH=50"
		QValStr = QValStr & "D=" & KaliteTable.GetAppName & "." & AlanAdi & vbCrLf
		MinMax = ""
		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i)) <> "" Then
			MinMax = "Min.:" & trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i))
		End if
		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i)) <> "" Then
			if trim(MinMax) <> "" Then MinMax = MinMax & " - "
			MinMax = MinMax & "Max.:" & trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i))
		End if
		if trim(MinMax) <> "" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 T='" & MinMax & "' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
 	QValStr = QValStr & "ENDFRAME"
	GetQValStr = QValStr
End Function
Sub FIELD_AV()
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")
	AlanAdi = Doc.GetGUIControl_Field()
	Set Q10T1 = Doc.Select2("wef23fsdf", Q10T.GetAppName, "VERIFIKASYONTEXT4='"& AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if Q10T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) = "" and trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) = "" Then Exit Sub
		
		AltLimit = -999999999999999999999999999999999999999999
		UstLimit = 9999999999999999999999999999999999999999999

		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" Then AltLimit = Doc.Cdbl(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1)
		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then UstLimit = Doc.Cdbl(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2)
	
		Deger = KaliteTable.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, -1)
		if Deger >= AltLimit and Deger <= UstLimit Then Exit Sub

		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" and trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & AltLimit & " ile " & UstLimit & " arasýnda olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Elseif trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & AltLimit & " den büyük veya eþit olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Elseif trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & UstLimit & " den küçük veya eþit olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		End if	
	End if
End Sub
Sub AlanAdlariniveEventleriEkle(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	For i = 1 to Q10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(Q10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = Q10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i) = AlanAdi

		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i)) <> "" or trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i)) <> "" Then
			KaliteTable.Setd7FieldEvent AlanAdi,"AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "FIELD_AV"
		End if
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KK
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()   
	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Yarimamul Tanitim Karti", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	Call KKEtiket(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End sub
Sub KKEtiket(pe1)
	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3


	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	if MTableT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 8.5, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 0.5, solMargin, 6, sagMargin - solMargin, "YARIMAMUL TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Gen1 = 13
	Aralik = 4.5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 11, solMargin + 1, 5, 8, "Tarih:")
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4.5, 12)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 6, "Kod")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 20, ": " & M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 7, "Taným")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, 10, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 18, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 12, "Ýþ Merk.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 15, ": " & trim(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 10, "Opr. :")
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4.5, 22)

	Set TECH10 = GetMuhendislik()
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, 17, "Talimatlar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Basla = pe1.GetTop
	Aralik1 = 3.5
	For i = 1 To TECH10.GetRowCount
		Tanim = TECH10.NOTES(i)		
		satirPozr = Basla + i * Aralik1
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 1, 5,sagMargin - Solmargin,i & ". " & Tanim)
	Next

	YaziAlt = AltMargin - 17.5

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 12, solMargin + 5, 12, 50, trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(1)) & " KK")
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 5, solMargin + 3, 5.5, 50, "CODE39", trim(MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(1)) & " KK", "")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(YaziAlt + 2, solMargin + 58, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 58)
	Ust = pe1.GetTop
	sol = pe1.GetLeft
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, AltMargin - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 0.3, sol, 5, sagMargin - sol, "Kalite Kontrol")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
Function GetMuhendislik()
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	SiraNo = M10T.R_SIRANO
	QStr = "TABLE1='MMPS10E'&KRITER='" & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO) & "'&KOD='" & trim(SiraNo) & "'"
	Set GetMuhendislik = Doc.Select1("sw2edccc", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1)
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KTA
Hangi Tablo için : STOK001
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Toplu KTA giriþi
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	AnaTabloStr = "LISTET"
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	AnaTablo.SafePack()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT)

	Call AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MTableT)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
	'EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
'	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 690
'	Else
'		En = En - 24
'		Boy = Boy - 60
'	End if 
	GridEn = En - 25
	GridBoy = Boy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Sipariþ Seviyeli MPS Yaratma" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'larý Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=30 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Açýlacak MPS 'leri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "MPS 'leri Yarat" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSOlustur')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	UDFStr = ""
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTableT)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)

		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Else
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar1 = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar1
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 or Alan = "KOD" or Alan = "AD" Then
				if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") = 0 Then
					AlanDegeri = LISTET.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				Else
					AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "STOK00")
					KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
					KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)

					AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", LISTET.KOD(i), KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
				End if
				MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", MTableT.KOD(i))
		Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
				KTA_Alan = Mid(Alan, 8)
				AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(KTA_Alan,-1,AlanDegeri)
				T_U1.Exec "SAVE" 
			End if
		Next 
	Next
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                           

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 

FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=600P WIDTH=1000P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS
SET CAPTION="Sipariþ Seviyeli MPS Yaratma"

MENUDEF
	POPUP TEXT="Dosya"
	MENUITEM TEXT="Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE
	ENDPOPUP
ENDMENUDEF

LOCATE TOP=1
//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '
//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM 

GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 WIDTH=970p HEIGHT=470p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P
	EDITTEXT D=KOD CAPTION="Stok Kodu" BROWSEBUTTON=1
	EDITTEXT D=AD CAPTION="Stok Adý"
	EDITTEXT D=SF_VRI_VR_R1 CAPTION="Þasi Numarasý"
	EDITTEXT D=FOKOD CAPTION="FO/C"
	EDITTEXT D=D_ID CAPTION="D.I.D."
	EDITTEXT D=SF_MIKTAR CAPTION="Miktar"
	COMBOBOX D=SF_SF_UNIT CAPTION="Birim"
	COMBOBOX D=REV_RTESTARIH CAPTION="Teslim Tarihi"
	EDITTEXT D=USTSEVIYE_KOD CAPTION="Üst Seviye Stok Kodu" BROWSEBUTTON=1
	EDITTEXT D=USTSEVIYE_AD CAPTION="Üst Seviye Stok Adý"

Sub Makro1()
	Set objXMLDoc = Nothing
	Set objXMLDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	objXMLDoc.async = False

	BaslangicTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.BuGun,1)
	For i = 0 to 45
		Tarih1 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(BaslangicTarihi, -1 * i)
		Call GetKurTable(Tarih1, objXMLDoc)
	Next
End Sub
Sub GetKurTable(ByVal Tarih, objXMLDoc)

	KURTarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Tarih, -1)
	Call HaftaSonuysaGeriAl(KURTarih )

	xmlTarih = Replace(Left(KURTarih ,7), "/", "") & "/" & Replace(Doc.StrToNorDate(KURTarih ), "/", "")

	objXMLDoc.Load "http://www.tcmb.gov.tr/kurlar/" & xmlTarih & ".xml"
'	objXMLDoc.Load "c:\today.xml"

	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	if Root is Nothing Then
'		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Kurlar alýnamadý " & xmlTarih, "Hata", MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set KurList = Root.getElementsByTagName("Currency")

	EXCRATE.EVRAKNOTARIH = Tarih

	EXCRATT.Empty    
	For Each Kur In KurList
		ParaBirimi = Kur.Attributes(1).NodeValue
		if trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("*PUNIT", "KOD", ParaBirimi, d7c_ErrMsgNo)) <> "" Then
			Birim = GetKurValue(Kur, "Unit")
			DovizAlis = GetKurValue(Kur, "ForexBuying")
			DovizSatis = GetKurValue(Kur, "ForexSelling")
			EfektifAlis = GetKurValue(Kur, "BanknoteBuying")
			EfektifSatis = GetKurValue(Kur, "BanknoteSelling")
		
			satirNo = EXCRATT.AddRow
			EXCRATT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			EXCRATT.CODEFROM(satirNo) = ParaBirimi
			EXCRATT.CODETO(satirNo) = Doc.DefaultPB
			EXCRATT.KURS_1(satirNo) = DovizSatis / Doc.CDbl(Birim)
			EXCRATT.KURS_3(satirNo) = DovizAlis / Doc.CDbl(Birim)
			EXCRATT.KURS_4(satirNo) = DovizSatis / Doc.CDbl(Birim)
			EXCRATT.KURS_5(satirNo) = EfektifAlis / Doc.CDbl(Birim)
			EXCRATT.KURS_6(satirNo) = EfektifSatis / Doc.CDbl(Birim)
		End if
	Next
	Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetKurValue(Kurxml, KurTipi)
	Set KurAlisSatisxml = Kurxml.SelectSingleNode(KurTipi)
	If Not KurAlisSatisxml Is Nothing Then
		If Not KurAlisSatisxml.FirstChild Is Nothing Then
			GetKurValue = KurAlisSatisxml.FirstChild.Data
		End If
	End If
End Function
Sub HaftaSonuysaGeriAl(ByRef Tarih)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate(Tarih)
	DOW = d7Date.GetDOW()
	if DOW = 6 or DOW = 7 Then
		d7Date.Increment(5 - DOW)
		Tarih = d7Date.GetRevDate
	End if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	l = ListedeMi("        29", "10..12;151..19;20;21;28..30", "R", 10)
	MSGBOX L
End Sub
Function ListedeMi(Kod, KontrolStr, Justify, Len)
	ListedeMi = False
	KontrolStr = trim(KontrolStr)
	if KontrolStr = "" Then
		ListedeMi = True
		Exit Function
	End if
	Arr1 = StrtoArray(KontrolStr, Justify, Len)
	For i = 0 to uBound(Arr1, 1)
		if Kod >= Arr1(i,0) and Kod <= Arr1(i,1) Then
			ListedeMi = True
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function StrtoArray(Str1, Justify, Len)
	Dim KodArray()

	KodArr1 = Split(Str1, ";")
	ub = UBound(KodArr1)
	ReDim KodArray(ub, 1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(KodArr1)
		KodArr11 = Split(KodArr1(i), "..")
		KodArray(i, 0) = Justify1(KodArr11(0), Justify, Len)
		if uBound(KodArr11) = 0 Then
			KodArray(i, 1) = Justify1(KodArr11(0), Justify, Len)
		Else
			KodArray(i, 1) = Justify1(KodArr11(1), Justify, Len)
		End if
	Next
	StrtoArray = KodArray
End Function
Function Justify1(Str1, Justify, Len)
	if Justify = "L" Then
		Justify1 = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Str1), Len)
	Elseif Justify = "R" Then
		Justify1 = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Str1), Len)
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARIH","D",10,0,"L","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing

Sub MaliyetAktar()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Tarih = MTable.TARIH
	if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK00 " & _
				"LEFT JOIN BOMU01E ON BOMU01E.MAMULCODE=STOK00.KOD AND STOK00.AP10='1' " & _
				"WHERE KOD<'9999999' AND BOMU01E.EVRAKNO IS NULL"

	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXX", QStr
	Set Tbl = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	For i = 1 to Tbl.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Standart Maliyet Tablosuna Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & Tbl.GetRowCount()
		Maliyet = Doc.SonHesaplanMaliyet(Tbl.KOD(i),"", Tarih)
		if Maliyet > 0 Then
			StokKodu = Tbl.KOD(i)
			EndeksKodu = "EN"
			UnsurKodu = "HM"
			Fiyat = Maliyet
			PB = Doc.DefaultPB
			Miktar = 1
			IslemBirimi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
			Call StandartMaliyetYaz(StokKodu, EndeksKodu, UnsurKodu, Tarih, Fiyat, PB, Miktar, IslemBirimi)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub StandartMaliyetYaz(StokKodu, EndeksKodu, UnsurKodu, Tarih, Fiyat, PB, Miktar, IslemBirimi)

	Set S0E = Doc.GetTableObject("STDMH0E")
	Set S0T = Doc.GetTableObject("STDMH0T")
	Set ST = Doc.Select1("ww", "STDMH0E","KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&STDMALTYPE='" & EndeksKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if ST.GetRowCount() = 0 then
		ret = Doc.New_Voucher()				
		S0E.STDMALTYPE = EndeksKodu
		S0E.KOD = StokKodu
		S0T.AddRow
		satirNo = S0T.GetRecCount
	else
		ret = Doc.Load_Voucher(ST.EVRAKNO)
		if ret <> 0 then
			k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
			exit sub
		end if
		satirNo = satirBul(S0T, UnsurKodu, Tarih)
		if satirNo = 0 then satirNo = S0T.AddRow
	end if

	TemelBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
	TemelFiyat = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(StokKodu, TemelBirim, IslemBirimi, d7c_ErrMsgNo) * Fiyat

	S0T.UNSUR(satirNo) = UnsurKodu
	S0T.TUTAR(satirNo) = TemelFiyat
	S0T.PARABIRIMI(satirNo) = PB
	S0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(satirNo) = Tarih
	ret = Doc.Save_Voucher()
	if ret <> 0 then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		ret = Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		exit sub
	end if
	ret = Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Function satirBul(S0T, UnsurKodu, Tarih)
	satirBul = 0
	For i = 1 to S0T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S0T.UNSUR(i)) = trim(UnsurKodu) and S0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(i) = Tarih then
			satirBul = i
			exit function
		end if
	Next
End Function
Function GKYoksaEkle(GrupKoduEvrakNo, GrupKodu, Ad)
	Set GST = Doc.Select1("ddee", "GECOUST", "EVRAKNO='" & GrupKoduEvrakNo & "'&KOD='" & GrupKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if GST.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("GECO10", "")
		Set GT = Doc.GetTableObject("GECOUST")

		Doc1.Load_Voucher GrupKoduEvrakNo
		satirNo = GT.AddRow
		GT.KOD(satirNo) = GrupKodu
		GT.AD(satirNo) = Ad
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
	GKYoksaEkle = GrupKodu
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "I" and _
				(Left(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i),1) = "K" or Left(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i),2) = "IZ")  Then
			Hiz = GetHiz(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i))
			if Hiz > 0 Then
				MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(i) 
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0 = Hiz
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetHiz(TezgahKodu, SiraNo)
	GetHiz = 0
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("def54y66", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & SiraNo & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	Set I00 = Doc.Select1("d4r5555", "IMLT00", "KOD='" & TezgahKodu & "'", "", -1)
	PVCBasmaKapasitesi = I00.B_MASRF_DGTM_KATS4 'kg/saat
	MaxCekiciHizi = I00.B_MASRF_DGTM_KATS5
	BazYogunluk = 1.62	

	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
'		PlastikYogunluk = GetPlastikYogunluk(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i))
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("de45g66", "STOK00", "KOD='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
		PlastikYogunluk = S00.B_YOGUNLUK
		if PlastikYogunluk > 0 Then
			YeniBasmaKapasitesi = PVCBasmaKapasitesi * PlastikYogunluk / BazYogunluk 'kg/saat
			Hiz = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR * YeniBasmaKapasitesi, M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i) * 60), 0) 'm/dk
			if Hiz > MaxCekiciHizi Then Hiz = MaxCekiciHizi
			GetHiz = Doc.Div(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, Hiz * 60)
		End if
	Next
End Function

Dim Silindi
Sub Makro1()
	Silindi = False
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE = "I" Then
			Set TECH10 = GetMuhendislik(i)
			Call MuhendislikTanimlariniMesajaYaz(MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i), TECH10)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMuhendislik(SatirNo)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu, MusteriKodu, MPSNo)

	Set GetMuhendislik = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
End Function
Sub MuhendislikTanimlariniMesajaYaz(rsiraNo, TECH10)
	if TECH10.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Doc.GNMESG_CREATEEDITTABLE "","MMPS10E", MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	Set Tbl_GNMESG2=Doc.GetTableObject("GNMESG2")

	If Not Silindi Then
		Tbl_GNMESG2.Empty
		Silindi = True
	End if

	For i = 1 to TECH10.GetRowCount()
		MuhendislikKodu = TECH10.TSCODE(i)
		if MuhendislikKodu <> EskiMuhendislikKodu Then
			MuhStr = GetMuhStr(TECH10, MuhendislikKodu)
			MuhendislikTanimiArr = GetYaziArr(trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", MuhendislikKodu, -1)) & " : " & MuhStr, 70)

			For j = 0 to uBound(MuhendislikTanimiArr)
				satirNo = Tbl_GNMESG2.AddRow
				Tbl_GNMESG2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				Tbl_GNMESG2.KOD = trim(rsiraNo)
				if j = 0 Then
					Tbl_GNMESG2.NOTES = MuhendislikTanimiArr(j)
				Else
					Tbl_GNMESG2.NOTES = ".." & MuhendislikTanimiArr(j)
				End if
			Next
		End if
		EskiMuhendislikKodu = MuhendislikKodu
	Next

	Doc.GNMESG_SAVE "MMPS10E", MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
End Sub
Function GetYaziArr(Yazi, StrLen)
	ReDim StrArr(0)
	Yazi = trim(Yazi)
	if Len(Yazi) <= StrLen Then
		StrArr(0) = Yazi
	else
		Do
			if Len(Yazi) <= StrLen Then
				StrArr(uBound(StrArr)) = Yazi
				Exit Do
			Else
				Bosluk = 0
				For i = (StrLen + 1) To 1 Step -1
					if Mid(Yazi, i, 1) = " " Then
						Bosluk = i
						Exit For
					End if
				Next
				if Bosluk = 0 then Bosluk = StrLen + 1
				StrArr(uBound(StrArr)) = Left(Yazi, Bosluk-1)
				Yazi = trim(Mid(Yazi, Bosluk))
				Redim Preserve StrArr(uBound(StrArr) + 1)
			End if
		Loop
	End if
	GetYaziArr = StrArr
End Function
Function GetMuhStr(TECH10, MuhendislikKodu)
	GetMuhStr = ""
	For i = 1 to TECH10.GetRowCount()
		if trim(TECH10.TSCODE(i)) = trim(MuhendislikKodu) Then
			GetMuhStr = GetMuhStr & trim(TECH10.TSTEXT(i)) & " "
		End if
	Next
	GetMuhStr = trim(GetMuhStr)
End Function

Makro Kodu : MUHENDISLI

Açýklama : Excel 'den Mühendislik Tanýmlarýný Aktar

Sub Makro1()
	Set SSS = doc.CreateSobject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\talimat.xls", False, True)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 3 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Mühendislik Tanýmlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		OperasyonKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value
	
		QStr = "KRITERCODE_1='SKOD'&KRITERVALUE_1='" & StokKodu & "'" & _
					"&KRITERCODE_2='OKOD'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & OperasyonKodu & "'"

		Set T10E = Doc.Select1("sded4ftr5", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
		if T10E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Doc.Load_Voucher T10E.EVRAKNO
			TECH10T.Empty
		Else
			Doc.New_Voucher
			TECH10E.KRITERCODE_1 = "SKOD"
			TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = StokKodu

			TECH10E.KRITERCODE_2 = "OKOD"
			TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_2 = OperasyonKodu
		End if

		For j = 4 to 255
			MuhendislikKodu = trim(Sh1.Cells(1,j).Value)
			if MuhendislikKodu = "" Then Exit For

			MuhendislikTanimi = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,j).Value)
			if trim(MuhendislikTanimi) <> "" Then
				MuhendislikTanimiArr = GetYaziArr(trim(MuhendislikTanimi), 70)
				For k = 0 to uBound(MuhendislikTanimiArr)
					satirNo = TECH10T.AddRow
					TECH10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
					TECH10T.TSCODE(satirNo) = MuhendislikKodu
					TECH10T.TSTEXT(satirNo) = MuhendislikTanimiArr(k)
				Next
			End if
		Next
		if trim(TECH10E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher	
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetYaziArr(Yazi, StrLen)
	ReDim StrArr(0)
	Yazi = trim(Yazi)
	if Len(Yazi) <= StrLen Then
		StrArr(0) = Yazi
	else
		Do
			if Len(Yazi) <= StrLen Then
				StrArr(uBound(StrArr)) = Yazi
				Exit Do
			Else
				Bosluk = 0
				For i = (StrLen + 1) To 1 Step -1
					if Mid(Yazi, i, 1) = " " Then
						Bosluk = i
						Exit For
					End if
				Next
				if Bosluk = 0 then Bosluk = StrLen + 1
				StrArr(uBound(StrArr)) = Left(Yazi, Bosluk-1)
				Yazi = trim(Mid(Yazi, Bosluk))
				Redim Preserve StrArr(uBound(StrArr) + 1)
			End if
		Loop
	End if
	GetYaziArr = StrArr
End Function

Function GetNetAgirlik(StokKodu, Miktar)
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M",StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()	
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			HammaddeKodu = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", HammaddeKodu, "")
			if trim(uCase(Birim)) = "KG" Then
				ToplamAgirlik = ToplamAgirlik + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetNetAgirlik = ToplamAgirlik
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Doc.LoadFirst_Voucher()
	Do
		For i = 1 to QVAL10T.GetRowCount()
			QVAL10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i) = Replace(QVAL10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i), ",", ".")
			QVAL10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i) = Replace(QVAL10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i), ",", ".")
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		Next
	Loop While Doc.LoadNext_Voucher = 0

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : PALET
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10

Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	'MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	'MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1=MMPS10TM(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 16, 0, "R", "", ""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "METRAJ", "DOUBLE", 16, 0, "R", "", ""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ETIKET_SAYISI", "DOUBLE", 16, 0, "R", "", ""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "BASILAN_ETIKET_SAYISI", "DOUBLE", 16, 0, "R", "", ""
			
	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL2=STACK","IO")
	Call AddMTable2Fields(MTableT2)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	Call FillMTableT()
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

End Sub
Sub FillMTableT()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	MTableT.Empty()

	Alanlar = M10TM.GetFieldNames("")

	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		For each alan in Alanlar
			alanDegeri = M10TM.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
		Next
		MTableT.MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.CDbl(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i), "MIKTAR", 0))
		MTableT.METRAJ(satirNo) = Doc.CDBl(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i), "BOY", 0))
		MTableT.BASILAN_ETIKET_SAYISI(satirNo) = GetBasilanEtiketSayisi(M10TM.EVRAKNO(i), M10TM.TRNUM(i))
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketYarat()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")
	If MTableT.ETIKET_SAYISI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7Msgbox "Yaratýlacak Etiket Sayýsý alaný boþ.", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	EtiketSayisi = MTableT.ETIKET_SAYISI

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK67", "")
	For i = 1 to EtiketSayisi
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiketler yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & EtiketSayisi
		Call EtiketKartiYarat(Doc1)
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	Call EtiketleriGoster()
End Sub
Sub EtiketKartiYarat(Doc1)
	Set S67E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK67E")
	Set S67T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK67T")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL2")
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	satirNo = S67T.AddRow	
	S67T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	S67T.MPSNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO
	if trim(S67T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then
		S67T.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM
	End if
	S67T.KOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU
	S67T.LOTNUMBER = "*"
	S67T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.MIKTAR * MTableT.METRAJ

	Doc1.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketleriGoster()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL2")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	
	MPSNo = MTableT.EVRAKNO
	MPSTrNum = MTableT.TRNUM
	if trim(MPSTrNum) = "" Then
		Doc.d7Msgbox "Üstteki 'Mamul' bölümünden istenen mamulu seçtikten sonra tekrar deneyiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	MTableT2.Empty

	Set S67T = Doc.Select1("sw223", "STOK67T", "MPSNO='" & MPSNo & "'&MPSTM_TRNUM='" & MPSTrNum & "'", "", -1)
	S67T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	For i = 1 to S67T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S67T.EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT2.TARIH(satirNo) = S67T.TARIH(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S67T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = S67T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddMTable2Fields(MTable2)
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SSCCNO", "CHAR", 12, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "TARIH", "DATE", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", "STUNIT"
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 16, 0, "R", "", ""
End Sub
Function GetBasilanEtiketSayisi(MPSNo, MPSTrNum)
	Set S67T = Doc.Select1("sw223", "STOK67T", "MPSNO='" & MPSNo & "'&MPSTM_TRNUM='" & MPSTrNum & "'", "", -1)
	S67T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	GetBasilanEtiketSayisi = S67T.GetRowCount()
End Function
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	sayfaBoyu	= 80
	sayfaEni = 100
	UstMargin = 4
	SolMargin = 3
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 3
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Palet Etiketi" & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	
	siraNo = 0
	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(MTableT2.SELECTED(i)) Then
			siraNo = siraNo  + 1
			if siraNo  > 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call PaletEtiket1Yazdir(pe1, i)
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call PaletEtiket2Yazdir(pe1, i)
		End if
	Next
	if siraNo > 0 Then
		res = pe1.Show()
		res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Etiketler bölümünden basýlacak etiketleri seçmelisiniz", "Uyarý", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Sub PaletEtiket1Yazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "Palet No")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 56)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3, solMargin, 28, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(MTableT2.EVRAKNO(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 22, solMargin, 6, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")))

	Gen = 23
	Aralik = 10
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 13, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen, "Metraj(m)")
	Ust = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, Gen)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen, MTableT.METRAJ)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, pe1.GetRight + Aralik, 5, Gen, "Miktar(adet)")
	Ust = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, Gen)
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen, MTableT.MIKTAR)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, pe1.GetRight + Aralik, 5, Gen, "Toplam(m)")
	Ust = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, Gen)
	res = pe1.UseSet(2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen, MTableT.METRAJ * MTableT.MIKTAR)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Sub PaletEtiket2Yazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL2")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEPL1")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "Palet No")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 56)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3, solMargin, 28, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(MTableT2.EVRAKNO(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 22, solMargin, 6, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")))

	Gen = 23
	Aralik = 10
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 10, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen, "Lot No")
	Ust = pe1.GetTop()
	Ara = 4.5
	For i = 1 to 7
		res = pe1.Line(Ust + (i - 1) * Ara + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, Gen)
	Next

	res = pe1.Write(Ust, pe1.GetRight + Aralik, 5, Gen, "Miktar(adet)")
	For i = 1 to 7
		res = pe1.Line(Ust + (i - 1) * Ara + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, Gen)
	Next

	res = pe1.Write(Ust, pe1.GetRight + Aralik, 5, Gen, "Barkod")
	For i = 1 to 7
		res = pe1.Line(Ust + (i - 1) * Ara + 4, pe1.GetLeft, 0, Gen)
	Next
End Sub

GROUPBOX TEXT="Etiketler" AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=5p TOP=E+5p WIDTH=1000p HEIGHT=425P BCKCOL=RGB_192_192_192 BORDER=SUNKEN TXTCOL=RGB_000_089_132 //SUNKEN //RAISED //ETCHED //BITMAP=BACK006 //BCKCOL=RGB_064_104_168

		CHECKBOX D="SELECTED" CAPTION="..." TRUEFALSE=EH
		EDITTEXT D=EVRAKNO  CAPTION="Palet No" NOEDIT=YES
		EDITTEXT D=SF_MIKTAR  CAPTION="Miktar" NOEDIT=YES

		EDITTEXT D=SF_UNIT CAPTION="Birim" NOEDIT=YES
		EDITTEXT D="TARIH" CAPTION="Tarih"  BROWSEBUTTON=1 NOEDIT=YES
	ENDGRID
ENDGROUPBOX

ENDFORM

Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
'	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 690
'	Else
'		En = En - 24
'		Boy = Boy - 60
'	End if 
	GridEn = En - 25
	GridBoy = Boy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Sipariþ Seviyeli MPS Yaratma" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'larý Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=30 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Açýlacak MPS 'leri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "MPS 'leri Yarat" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSOlustur')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "STOK60T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
		Grupkodu = ""
		Tablo = ""
		Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
		Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "STOK60T")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
		Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
		Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
		AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
		Grupkodu = ""
		Tablo = ""
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " BROWSEBUTTON=1 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " BROWSEBUTTON=1 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = STOK60T.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set ss = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK2A", "")
	Set S2AE = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK2AE")
	Set S2AT = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK2AT")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S2AE.TARIH = STOK2AE.TARIH
	S2AE.AMBCODE = STOK2AE.AMBCODE

	For i = 1 to STOK2AT.GetRowCount()
		ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", STOK2AT.KOD(i),1,STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(i),"","","","","","","","") 		
		Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
		if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Doc.d7MsgBox STOK2AT.KOD(i) & " ürün aðacý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		For j = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
			if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "H" Then
				satirNo = S2AT.AddRow()
				S2AT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				S2AT.KOD = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j)
				S2AT.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.Round(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j), 3)
			End if
STOK2AT.LOTNUMBER(i) = "x"
		Next
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Dim S01Arr, S01Arr1
Sub Makro1()
	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"SF_VRI_VR_RC", "SF_VRI_VR_R1", "SF_VRI_VR_R2", "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "SF_VRI_NUM1", "SF_VRI_NUM2", "SF_SF_UNIT")
	S01Arr1 = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT")

	Call GirisCikisFisleriOlustur()
	'Call KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
End Sub
Sub GirisCikisFisleriOlustur()
	Set S99 = Doc.Select2("_S99", "SAYM99T", "", "KOD", -1)
	S99.GroupBy("KOD")
	Set S99G = Doc.Select2("_S99G", "_S99", "SF_FARK>0", "", -1)
	Set S99C = Doc.Select2("_S99C", "_S99", "SF_FARK<0", "", -1)

	if S99G.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Call GirisFisiniOlustur(S99G)
	if S99C.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Call CikisFisiniOlustur(S99C)
End Sub
Sub GirisFisiniOlustur(S99G)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK24", "")
	Set S24E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24E")	
	Set S24T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24T")	
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S24E.AMBCODE = "41MA"

	For i = 1 to S99G.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.DRound(S99G.SF_FARK(i)) > 0 Then
			satirNo = S24T.AddRow()
			S24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For j = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				AlanAdi = S01Arr(j)
				S99GDeger = S99G.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				S24T.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, S99GDeger
			Next
			S24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.DRound(S99G.SF_FARK(i))
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub CikisFisiniOlustur(S99C)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK21", "")
	Set S21E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")	
	Set S21T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")	
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S21E.AMBCODE = "41MA"

	For i = 1 to S99C.GetRowCount()
		CikacakMiktar = Doc.DRound(S99C.SF_FARK(i))
		if CikacakMiktar < 0 Then
			DepoStr = SAYM99E.DEPO1 & SAYM99E.DEPO2 & SAYM99E.DEPO3 & SAYM99E.DEPO4 & SAYM99E.DEPO5 & SAYM99E.DEPO6 & _
							SAYM99E.DEPO7 & SAYM99E.DEPO8
			if trim(DepoStr) = "" Then
				DepoStr = "'&AMBCODE>'T'&AMBCODE<'TZZZZZ'"
				Set S01 = Doc.Select1("_S01", "STOK01", "KOD='" & S99C.KOD(i) & DepoStr, "", -1)
			Else
				Set S01 = Doc.Select1("_S01", "STOK01", "KOD='ssw4444bvhgf'", "", -1)
				For ni = 1 to 8
					DepoKodu = SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("DEPO" & ni, 1)
					if trim(DepoKodu) <> "" Then
						DepoStr = "'&AMBCODE='" & DepoKodu & "'"
						Set S01X = Doc.Select1("_S01X", "STOK01", "KOD='" & S99C.KOD(i) & DepoStr, "", -1)
						S01.AppendTable(S01X)
					End if
				Next
			End if
			KalanMiktar = Abs(CikacakMiktar)
			For k = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
				if S01.SF_MIKTAR(k) > KalanMiktar Then
					CikanMiktar = KalanMiktar
				Else
					CikanMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(k)
				End if
				KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - CikanMiktar
				satirNo = S21T.AddRow()
				S21T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				For j = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
					AlanAdi = S01Arr(j)
					S01AlanAdi = S01Arr1(j)
					S01Deger = S01.GetFieldValue(S01AlanAdi, k)
					S21T.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, S01Deger
				Next
				S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.DRound(CikanMiktar)
				S21T.AMBCODE_TR(satirNo) = S01.AMBCODE(k)
				if KalanMiktar = 0 Then Exit For				
			Next
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
End Sub

Dim S01Arr, OldS01Arr, NewS01Arr
Sub Makro1()
	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"SF_VRI_VR_RC", "SF_VRI_VR_R1", "SF_VRI_VR_R2", "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "SF_VRI_NUM1", "SF_VRI_NUM2", "SF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE")

	OldS01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"OSF_VRI_VR_C", "OSF_VRI_VR_R1", "OSF_VRI_VR_R2", "OSF_VRI_VR_R3", "OSF_VRI_NUM1", "OSF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE_TR")


	NewS01Arr = Array("NEWCODE", "NEWLOTNUMBER", "NEWSERINO", "NEWLOCATION1", "NEWLOCATION2", "NEWLOCATION3", "NEWLOCATION4", "NEWFOKOD", _
					"PN_SF_VRI_VR_C", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM1", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "TARGETAMBCODE_TR")

	Call KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set S99LISTE = Doc.Select2("_S99L", "SAYM99T", "SF_MIKTAR_01>0&SF_FARK<>0", "KOD", -1)
	Set S99SONUC = Doc.Select2("_S99S", "SAYM99T", "SF_MIKTAR_11>0&SF_FARK<>0", "KOD", -1)
	if S99LISTE.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = "41MA"
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "41MA"

	EskiKod = ""
	For i = 1 to S99LISTE.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kod/Lot/Seri transfer fiþi yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S99LISTE.GetRowCount()
		if S99LISTE.KOD(i) <> EskiKod Then
			Set SONUC = Doc.Select2("ddee", "_S99S", "KOD='" & S99LISTE.KOD(i) &"'", "", -1)
			DagitimSatir = 1
		End if
		ListeMiktar = S99LISTE.SF_MIKTAR_01(i)
		For j = DagitimSatir to SONUC.GetRowCount()
			Cikis = False
			SonucMiktar = SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j)
			if SonucMiktar > ListeMiktar Then
				DagitilacakMiktar = ListeMiktar
				SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) = SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j)  - DagitilacakMiktar

				Cikis = True
			Else
				DagitilacakMiktar = SonucMiktar
				ListeMiktar = ListeMiktar - SonucMiktar
				SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) = 0
				DagitimSatir = DagitimSatir + 1
				if ListeMiktar = 0 Then Cikis = True
			End if

			satirNo = S25T.AddRow
			S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For k = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				S25TOldAlan = OldS01Arr(k)
				S01Alan = S01Arr(k)
				S25T.SetFieldValue S25TOldAlan, satirNo, S99LISTE.GetFieldValue(S01Alan,i)
			Next
			S25T.SF_MIKTAR = DagitilacakMiktar

			For k = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				S25TNewAlan = NewS01Arr(k)
				S01Alan = S01Arr(k)
				S25T.SetFieldValue S25TNewAlan, satirNo, SONUC.GetFieldValue(S01Alan,j)
			Next

			if Cikis Then Exit For
		Next
		EskiKod = S99LISTE.KOD(i)
	Next
	Call EtkisizSatirlariSil(S25T)
	'Call SendTableToExcel(S25T)
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtkisizSatirlariSil(ByRef S25T)
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etkisiz satýrlar siliniyor : " & i & " / " & S25T.GetRowCount()
		EskiDeger = ""
		YeniDeger = ""
		For k = 0 to uBound(OldS01Arr)
			S25TOldAlan = OldS01Arr(k)
			S25TNewAlan = NewS01Arr(k)
			EskiDeger = EskiDeger & S25T.GetFieldValue(S25TOldAlan, i)
			YeniDeger = YeniDeger & S25T.GetFieldValue(S25TNewAlan, i)
		Next
		if YeniDeger = EskiDeger Then
			S25T.KOD(i) = ""
			S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
