Sub CikilacaklariGetirveHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set CIKIS = Doc.GetTableObject("CIKISLAR")

	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MAKROTABLET")
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	For i = 1 to CIKIS.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & CIKIS.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For j = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
			AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
			Deger = CIKIS.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, Deger
		Next
		MTableT.SF_FARK = Abs(MTableT.SF_FARK)
		BirimAgirlik = GetBirimAgirlik(MTableT.KOD)
		MTableT.BIRIM_AGIRLIK = BirimAgirlik(0)
		MTableT.BAKIR_BIRIM_AGIRLIK = BirimAgirlik(1)
		MTableT.PVC_BIRIM_AGIRLIK = BirimAgirlik(2)
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_GK_7_AD = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK7", "AD", MTableT.KOD_STOK00_GK_7, -1)
	Next
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub DagitimlariYap()
	Set MTableHrd = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEHRD")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	For i = 1 to MTableHrd.GetRowCount()
		KalanMiktar = MTableHrd.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableHrd.SetCurrentRow(i)
		MTableHrd.DAGITIM_MIKTAR = 0
		For j = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if trim(MTableHrd.KOD(i)) = trim(MTableT.KOD_STOK00_GK_7(j)) Then
				if KalanMiktar <= MTableT.TOPLAM_AGIRLIK(j) Then
					DagitimMiktari = KalanMiktar
				Else
					DagitimMiktari = MTableT.TOPLAM_AGIRLIK(j)
				End if
				MTableT.SetCurrentRow(j)
				MTableT.HURDA_AGIRLIK = DagitimMiktari

				MTableHrd.DAGITIM_MIKTAR = MTableHrd.DAGITIM_MIKTAR + DagitimMiktari

				KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - DagitimMiktari

				if KalanMiktar = 0 Then Exit For
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub HurdalariGetir()
	Set MTableHrd = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEHRD")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hurda miktarlarý hesaplanýyor..."

	QStr = "EVRAKTYPE='DT'&NITELIK='HU'&AMBCODE='41HR'&TARIH>='" & MTable.B_TARIH & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&TARIH<='" & MTable.E_TARIH & "'"
	End if
	Set S10A = Doc.Select1("dde456", "STOK10A", QStr, "", -1)
	S10A.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTableHrd.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableHrd.Empty
	For i = 1 to S10A.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Yazýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S10A.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableHrd.AddRow()
		MTableHrd.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableHrd.KOD = S10A.KOD(i)
		MTableHrd.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S10A.KOD(i), "")
		MTableHrd.SF_MIKTAR = S10A.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableHrd.SF_UNIT = S10A.SF_UNIT(i)
	Next
	MTableHrd.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub HurdaDagitimKalanlariHesapla()
	Set MTableHrd = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEHRD")
	sNo = MTableHrd.GetCurrentRow()

	For i = 1 to MTableHrd.GetRowCount()
		MTableHrd.SetCurrentRow(i)
		Set MT = Doc.Select2("de222222", "MAKROTABLET", "KOD_STOK00_GK_7='" & trim(MTableHrd.KOD) & "'", "", -1)
		if MT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MT.GroupBy2("KOD_STOK00_GK_7")
			MTableHrd.DAGITIM_MIKTAR = MT.HURDA_AGIRLIK
		Else
			MTableHrd.DAGITIM_MIKTAR = 0
		End if
	Next	
	MTableHrd.SetCurrentRow(sNo)
End Sub
Sub GirisCikisFisiOlustur()
	Call HurdaCikisFisiOlustur()
	Call VaryansFisiOlustur()
End Sub
Sub HurdaCikisFisiOlustur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK21", "")
	Set S21E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
	Set S21T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S21E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
	S21E.NITELIK = "HC"

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.HURDA_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			satirNo = S21T.AddRow
			S21T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S21T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR = MTableT.HURDA_MIKTAR(i)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Sub VaryansFisiOlustur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK21", "")
	Set S21E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
	Set S21T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S21E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
	S21E.NITELIK = "IV"

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.VARYANS_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			satirNo = S21T.AddRow
			S21T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S21T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR = MTableT.VARYANS_MIKTAR(i)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Function GFLSMik_Round(Sayi)
	GFLS_Mik = Doc.Customp_Gets("GFLS_MIK")
	Nokta = InStr(1, GFLS_Mik, ".")
	Ondalik = Doc.CDbl(trim(Mid(GFLS_Mik, Nokta + 1)))
	GFLSMik_Round = Doc.Round(Sayi, Ondalik)
End Function


'/*** Event ***
Sub BIRIM_AGIRLIK_AV()
	Call ToplamAgirlikHesapla()
	Call VaryansMiktarHesapla()
End Sub
Sub SF_FARK_AV()
	Call ToplamAgirlikHesapla()
	Call BakirAgirligiHesapla()
	Call PVCAgirligiHesapla
	Call VaryansMiktarHesapla
End Sub
Sub BAKIR_BIRIM_AGIRLIK_AV()
	Call BakirAgirligiHesapla()
End Sub
Sub PVC_BIRIM_AGIRLIK_AV()
	Call PVCAgirligiHesapla
End Sub
Sub HURDA_AGIRLIK_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	If Doc.SigDig(MTableT.SF_FARK) <= 3 Then
		Ondalik = 3
	Else
		Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(MTableT.SF_FARK)
	End if
	if Ondalik > 6 Then Ondalik = 6
	MTableT.HURDA_MIKTAR = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(MTableT.HURDA_AGIRLIK * MTableT.SF_FARK, MTableT.TOPLAM_AGIRLIK), Ondalik)
	Call VaryansMiktarHesapla()
End Sub
Sub HURDA_MIKTAR_AV()
	Call VaryansMiktarHesapla()
End Sub

Sub ToplamAgirlikHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	MTableT.TOPLAM_AGIRLIK = MTableT.SF_FARK * MTableT.BIRIM_AGIRLIK
End Sub
Sub BakirAgirligiHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	MTableT.BAKIR_AGIRLIK = MTableT.SF_FARK * MTableT.BAKIR_BIRIM_AGIRLIK
End Sub
Sub PVCAgirligiHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	MTableT.PVC_AGIRLIK = MTableT.SF_FARK * MTableT.PVC_BIRIM_AGIRLIK
End Sub
Sub VaryansMiktarHesapla()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	MTableT.VARYANS_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_FARK - MTableT.HURDA_MIKTAR
End Sub

Sub DAGITIM_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTableHrd = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEHRD")
	MTableHrd.KALAN = MTableHrd.SF_MIKTAR - MTableHrd.DAGITIM_MIKTAR
End Sub


'***/ Event *** 
Function GetBirimAgirlik(StokKodu)
	Dim AgirlikArray(2)
	AgirlikArray(0) = 0
	AgirlikArray(1) = 0
	AgirlikArray(2) = 0

	par1 = StokKodu
	par2 = 1
	par3 = "11..140099;15..16;200005..21;22..34"
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.CALC_BOM", "Calc_Bom3", par1, par2, par3)
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
			if UCase(trim(Birim)) = "KG" Then
				kgMiktar = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			Else
				kgMiktar = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Birim, "Kg", d7c_ERRMSG_NO) * Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
			if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >="1" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <= "2" Then
				AgirlikArray(1) = AgirlikArray(1) + kgMiktar
			Elseif Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >="22" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <= "34" Then
				AgirlikArray(2) = AgirlikArray(2) + kgMiktar
			End if
			AgirlikArray(0) = AgirlikArray(0) + kgMiktar
		End if
	Next
	GetBirimAgirlik = AgirlikArray
End Function

Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu :

FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=680P WIDTH=1015P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS

DEFINEFONT ID="default_groupbox_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
SET DEFAULTFONT_GROUPBOX=default_groupbox_font

SET CAPTION="Sayým Çýkýþ Ýþlemleri"

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SILA
Hangi Tablo için : MMPSR5
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		MMPS10T_LIST.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "I"
		MMPS10T_LIST.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) = "AK1"

		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "ACIK_KAPALI", MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i), "") = "K" Then
			MMPS10T_LIST.SetCurrentRow(i)
			MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i) = Space(10)
			MMPS10T_LIST.R_STATUS(i) = ""
			MMPS10T_LIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i) = ""
			MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i) = ""
			MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU_STOK00_AD(i) = ""
			MMPS10T_LIST.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = ""
			MMPS10T_LIST.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) = ""
			MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i) = 0
			MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_SF_URETILEN(i) = 0
			MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR(i) = 0
			For j = 1 to 30
				MMPS10T_LIST.SetFieldValue "COLVT" & Right("000" & j, 3), i, ""
			Next
			MMPS10T_LIST.COLVT030(i) = "zzzz"
		End if
	Next
	MMPS10T_LIST.Sort("COLVT030+EVRAKNO")
	MMPS10T_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIPAC
Hangi Tablo için : UPD00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Toplu Sipariþ Açma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	Set S40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	For i = 1 to STOK40T.GetRowCount()
		if STOK40T.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiSipNo Then
			if i <> 1 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK40T.EVRAKNO(i)
		End if
		For j = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
			if S40T.ARTNO(j) = STOK40T.ARTNO(i) Then
				S40T.ACIKKAPALI(j) = ""
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
		EskiSipNo = STOK40T.EVRAKNO(i)
	Next
	if trim(S40E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIPGETIR
Hangi Tablo için : STOK19
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call SipGetir()
End Sub
Sub SipGetir()
	Set AcikSipTable = GetAcikSip()
	Set SevkEmriTable = GetSevkEmriTable()

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESP")	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Alanlar = Array("ACIKKAPALI", "EVRAKNO", "ARTNO", "TARIH", "MUSTERIKODU", "KOD", _
					"SF_MIKTAR", "SF_SF_UNIT", "NOTES", "RTESTARIH", "REV_RTESTARIH", "TOLERANSTYPE", _
					"TOLERANS", "SF_PMIKTAR", "SF_IMIKTAR", "SF_NETKAPANANMIK")
	For i = 1 to AcikSipTable.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		For each alan in Alanlar
			Deger = AcikSipTable.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, Deger
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(satirNo), "")
		MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo), "")
	Next

	For i = 1 to SevkEmriTable.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		For each alan in Alanlar
			Deger = SevkEmriTable.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, Deger
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(satirNo), "")
		MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU(satirNo), "")
	Next
End Sub
Function GetAcikSip()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set S19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")
	Set GetAcikSip = Doc.Select1("se324443s", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' '&MUSTERIKODU='" & S19E.CHKODU & "'&KOD='" & MTable.KOD & "'", "", -1)
End Function
Function GetSevkEmriTable()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set S19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("de3333", "STOK01", "KOD='" & MTable.KOD & "'&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='SV'", "", -1)
	S01.GroupBy("ALLOC_SIPARTNO")

	Doc.CreateTableObject "de33sssa25r=STOK40T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set GetSevkEmriTable = Doc.GetTableObject("de33sssa25r")
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i), "ARTNO") = S19E.CHKODU Then
			Set S40T = Doc.Select1("dse3c5f5", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S40T.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)

			GetSevkEmriTable.AppendTable(S40T)
		End if
	Next
End Function

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SQL
Hangi Tablo için : REP17S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Rapor SQL komutunu yarat
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	REP17RT.SafePack
	REP17JT.SafePack
	REP17KT.SafePack
	SQLStr = ""

	SelectStr = GetSelectStr()
	SQLStr = SQLStr & SelectStr

	JoinStr = GetJoinStr()
	if trim(JoinStr) <> "" Then
		SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & JoinStr
	End if

	WhereStr = GetWhereStr()
	if trim(WhereStr) <> "" Then
		SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & WhereStr
	End if

	REP17SE_B.SQLTEXT = SQLStr
End Sub
Function GetSelectStr()
	SQLStr = "SELECT "
	k = 1
	For i = 1 to REP17RT.GetRowCount()
		if IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) and not EklenenAlanmi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
			if BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
				Alan = GetOrijinalAlanAdi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & " " & trim(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i))
			Else
				Alan = trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE) & "." & trim(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i))
			End if
			if Len(SQLStr) > k * 90 Then
				SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & Chr(9)
				k = k + 1
			End if
			SQLStr = SQLStr & trim(Alan) & ", "
		End if
	Next
	For i = 1 to REP17KT.GetRowCount()
		if not RaporIcerigindeVarmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
			if IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) and not EklenenAlanmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
				if BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
					Alan = GetOrijinalAlanAdi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & " " & trim(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i))
				Else
					Alan = trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE) & "." & trim(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i))
				End if
				if Len(SQLStr) > k * 90 Then
					SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & Chr(9)
					k = k + 1
				End if
				SQLStr = SQLStr & trim(Alan) & ", "
			End if
		End if
	Next

	if Right(trim(SQLStr), 1) = "," Then
		SQLStr = Left(SQLStr, Len(SQLStr) - 2)
	End if
	
	GetSelectStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & "FROM " & trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE)
End Function
Function GetWhereStr()
	if REP17KT.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		GetWhereStr = ""
		Exit Function
	End if
	WhereStr = "WHERE 1=1 "
	For i = 1 to REP17KT.GetRowCount()
		if IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) and not EklenenAlanmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) _
				and Doc.Is_EY1(REP17KT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
			WStr = CreateWhereStr(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i), REP17KT.KRITERTIPI(i))
			if WStr <> "" Then
				WhereStr = WhereStr & vbcrlf & WStr
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetWhereStr = WhereStr
End Function
Function CreateWhereStr(AlanAdi, KriterTipi)
	if not BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(AlanAdi) Then
		AlanAdi1 = trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE) & "." & trim(AlanAdi)
	Else
'		AlanAdi1 = trim(AlanAdi)
		AlanAdi1 = GetOrijinalAlanAdi(AlanAdi)
	End if
	if trim(KriterTipi) = "><" Then
		CreateWhereStr = " AND " & AlanAdi1 & ">=###KRITERGIRIS.B_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = ">=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = " AND " & AlanAdi1 & ">=###KRITERGIRIS.B_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = ">" Then
		CreateWhereStr = " AND " & AlanAdi1 & ">###KRITERGIRIS.B_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "<=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "<" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<###KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "<>" or trim(KriterTipi) = "!=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.NE_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<>###KRITERGIRIS.NE_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "BOÞ" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND " & AlanAdi1 & "=''"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "!BOÞ" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<>''"
	Elseif Left(KriterTipi,1) = "S" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND (" & GetCLStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1) & ")"
	Elseif Left(KriterTipi,2) = "NS" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND (" & GetNSStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1) & ")"
	End if
End Function
Function GetJoinStr()
	GetJoinStr = ""
	if IcmaldenOnceJoinYok Then Exit Function

	JoinStr = ""
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
			if JoinStr <> "" Then
				JoinStr = JoinStr & vbcrlf & "INNER JOIN "
			Else
				JoinStr = "INNER JOIN "
			End if
			JoinStr = JoinStr & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
			JoinStr = JoinStr & " ON "
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
			JoinStr = JoinStr & "."
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17JT.JOINEDFIELD(i))
			JoinStr = JoinStr & "="
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE)
			JoinStr = JoinStr & "."
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i))
		End if
	Next
	GetJoinStr = JoinStr
End Function
Function IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(AlanAdi)
	IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi = True
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		Baglanti = trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & "_" & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
		if len(Baglanti) > Len(AlanAdi) Then
			Exit Function
		Else
			if Left(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)) = Baglanti and Doc.Is_HN0(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
				IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi = False				
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(AlanAdi)
	BaglantidanGelenAlanmi = False
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		Baglanti = trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & "_" & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
		if len(Baglanti) > Len(AlanAdi) Then
			Exit Function
		Else
			if Left(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)) = Baglanti and Doc.Is_EY1(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
				BaglantidanGelenAlanmi = True
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetOrijinalAlanAdi(AlanAdi)
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		Baglanti = trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & "_" & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
		if Left(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)) = Baglanti Then
			TabloAdi = trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
			BaglantidanGelenAlan = Mid(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)+2)
			GetOrijinalAlanAdi = TabloAdi & "." & trim(BaglantidanGelenAlan)
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function EklenenAlanmi(AlanAdi)
	EklenenAlanmi = False
	For i = 1 to REP17UT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(REP17UT.FIELDNAME1(i)) = trim(AlanAdi) Then
			EklenenAlanmi = True
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function IcmaldenOnceJoinYok()
	IcmaldenOnceJoinYok = True
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
			IcmaldenOnceJoinYok = False				
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function RaporIcerigindeVarmi(AlanAdi)
	RaporIcerigindeVarmi = False
	For i = 1 to REP17RT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) = trim(AlanAdi) Then
			RaporIcerigindeVarmi = True
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetCLStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1)
	AlanSayisi = Doc.CDbl(Mid(KriterTipi, 2))
	OrStr = "1=0 " & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	For i = 1 to AlanSayisi
		OrStr = OrStr & "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"OR " & AlanAdi1 & "=###KRITERGIRIS.CL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"###ENDIF###" & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	Next
	GetCLStr = OrStr
End Function
Function GetNSStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1)
	AlanSayisi = Doc.CDbl(Mid(KriterTipi, 3))
	NSStr = "1=1 " & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	For i = 1 to AlanSayisi
		NSStr = NSStr & "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.NCL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<>###KRITERGIRIS.NCL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"###ENDIF###" & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	Next
	GetNSStr = NSStr
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : UAGAC
Hangi Tablo için : STOK19
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ürün Aðacýný Parçala
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set hmdTable = GetHmdTable()
	Call SendTableToExcel(hmdTable)
End Sub
Function GetHmdTable()
	Set bem = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set BomTable = GetBomTable()
	TableStr = BomTable.GetAppName()
	Set HmdTable = Doc.Select2("de3ft5", TableStr, "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to HmdTable.GetRowCount() 
		GK_9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_9", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
		if trim(GK_9) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & " Stok Kartý Grup Kodu-9 boþ", "", MB_OK
			HmdTable.ACIKLAMA(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", HmdTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
		Else
			HmdTable.ACIKLAMA(i) = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK9", "AD", GK_9, -1)
		End if
	Next
	HmdTable.GroupBy("ACIKLAMA")
'	HmdTable.GroupBy("BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
	Set GetHmdTable = HmdTable
End Function
Function GetBomTable()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "BMR1=WBMRSYS", "TMPCOPY"
	Set GetBomTable = Doc.GetTableObject("BMR1")
	Set S19T1 = Doc.Select2("sd33", "STOK19T", "", "", -1)
	S19T1.GroupBy2("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S19T1.GetRowCount()
		Set BomTable = Calc_Bom2(S19T1.KOD(i), S19T1.SF_MIKTAR(i), "22", "34")
		if BomTable.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetBomTable.AppendTable(BomTable)
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox S19T1.KOD(i) & " ürün aðacý bulunamýyor", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function Calc_Bom2(StokKodu, Miktar, StopStokKodu1, StopStokKodu2)
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		

	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Not ((Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(i) = 0 and _
							(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_CODE(i) < StopStokKodu1 or Tbmrsys.BOMREC_CODE(i) > StopStokKodu2)) or _
			(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) >= StopStokKodu1 and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <= StopStokKodu2)) Then
			Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
			Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	Tbmrsys.SafePack()
	Set Calc_Bom2 = Tbmrsys
End Function
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gÃ¶nderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu("MAKROTABLET")
	AddField2List = GetAddField2Str("MTableT", "MAKROTABLET")
	Mesaj = "Ekran Formu : " & vbcrlf & EkranFormu & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "AddField2 : " & vbcrlf & AddField2List
	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", Mesaj)
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(GridTabloAdi)
	En = 1000
	Boy = 690
	TabEn = En - 25
	TabBoy = Boy - 25

	GridEn = TabEn - 25
	GridBoy = TabBoy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Barkod Etiket Basýmý" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "TABDEF X=0p Y=+0p WIDTH=" & TabEn & "p H=" & TabBoy & "p AUTOSIZE=N" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Etiket Adetleri Giriþ"" CAPTION="" Etiket Adetleri Giriþ """ & vbCrLf
'	FormStr = FormStr & 	"TAB Name=""Etiket Yaratma"" CAPTION="" Etiket Yaratma """ & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTABDEF" & vbCrLf

'**
	FormStr = FormStr & "TAB ""Etiket Adetleri Giriþ""" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=30p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=" & GridTabloAdi & " HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Etiketleri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','EtiketleriHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDTAB" & vbCrLf


	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf
'	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	UDFStr = ""
'	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='" & KTA_Adi & "'", "", -1)
	Set U10T = Doc.GetTableObject("UFLD10T")
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Function GetAddField2Str(TabloAdi, TabloAdiStr)
	TableStr = "Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject(""" & TabloAdiStr & "=STACK"",""IO"")" & vbcrlf

	Set U10T = Doc.GetTableObject("UFLD10T")
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			Str1 = TabloAdi & ".Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""DATE"",10,0,""L"","""","""""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			Str1 = TabloAdi & ".Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""CHAR""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""L"","""",Grupkodu"
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			Str1 = TabloAdi & ".Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""CHAR""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""L"",""F2=DO_BRW('"" & Tablo & ""')"","""""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			Str1 = TabloAdi & ".Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""CHAR""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""L"","""","""""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			Str1 = TabloAdi & ".Addfield2 """ & AlanAdi & """, ""DOUBLE""," & Uzunluk & "," & Ondalik & ",""R"","""","""""
		End if
		TableStr = TableStr & Chr(9) & Str1 & vbcrlf
	Next
	GetAddField2Str = TableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(fso.GetSpecialFolder(2) & "\kod.xls", False)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	For i = 2 to 67
		uKodu = Replace(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value, "Geçersiz Kod = ", "")
		if trim(uKodu) <> "" Then
			Doc.Load_Voucher Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			satirNo = STOK0U.AddRow
			STOK0U.UCODE(satirNo) = uKodu
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	dosyaAdi = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2) & "\uretim.xls" 
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(dosyaAdi, False)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	
	For i = 2 to 65000
		if Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value = "" Then Exit For
		Tarih = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		Tarih = Day(Tarih) & "/" & Month(Tarih) & "/" & Year(Tarih)
		if Tarih <> EskiTarih Then
			if trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.New_Voucher
			STOK20E.AMBCODE = "41MA"
			STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "41KB"
			STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "41KB"
			STOK20E.TARIH = Tarih
		End if
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.KOD = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value

		EskiTarih = Tarih
	Next
	if trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK26E.TARGETAMBCODE = "41KB"
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : YM-1
Hangi Tablo için : SAYM11
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	For i = 1 to MSYM.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MSYM.OPERASYON(i)) <> "" Then
			Call ParcalaVeEkle(i)
		Else
			Call Aktar(i)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub ParcalaVeEkle(sSatirNo)
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")

	Set oPar = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	oPar.SetParameter "SAYIMRECETESI","1"
	oPar.SetParameter "SAYIMOPERASYONU", MSYM.OPERASYON(sSatirNo)
 	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom_Par("1", "M", MSYM.KOD(sSatirNo), 1, MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(sSatirNo),"","","","","","","","", oPar)

	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			satirNo = S11T.AddRow
			S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S11T.KOD = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			S11T.SF_MIKTAR = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(sSatirNo)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub Aktar(sSatirNo)
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	satirNo = S11T.AddRow

	S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	S11T.KOD = MSYM.KOD(sSatirNo)
	S11T.LOTNUMBER = MSYM.LOTNUMBER(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SERINO = MSYM.SERINO(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R1(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R2(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R3(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_NUM1(sSatirNo)

	S11T.SF_MIKTAR = MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(sSatirNo)
	S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(sSatirNo)
End Sub

Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : YMSAYIM

Public 	sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	'MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	'MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM=SAYM11T(NOFILE)","IO")
	'MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('BOMU01T','=BOMREC_OPERASYON','BOMREC_CODE+AKTIF_PASIF','BRW_BOMU01T_0',,""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'&&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=='I'"")",""
'	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('BOMU01T','=BOMREC_OPERASYON','BOMREC_CODE+AKTIF_PASIF','BRW_BOMU01T_0',,""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'&&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=='I'"",""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'"",""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'"",""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'"")",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT01')",""
			
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	Call FillMTableT()
'	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

End Sub
Sub FillMTableT()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	MTableT.Empty()

	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	Alanlar = S11T.GetFieldNames("")

	For i = 1 to S11T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		For each alan in Alanlar
			alanDegeri = S11T.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
		Next
		MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = ""
		MTableT.OPERASYON(satirNo) = S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Parcala()
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	For i = 1 to MSYM.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MSYM.OPERASYON(i)) <> "" Then
			Call ParcalaVeEkle(i)
		Else
			Call Aktar(i)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub ParcalaVeEkle(sSatirNo)
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	S11T.SafePack()

	Set oPar = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	oPar.SetParameter "SAYIMRECETESI","1"
	oPar.SetParameter "SAYIMOPERASYONU", MSYM.OPERASYON(sSatirNo)
 	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom_Par("1", "M", MSYM.KOD(sSatirNo), 1, MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(sSatirNo),"","","","","","","","", oPar)

	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			satirNo = S11T.AddRow
			S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S11T.KOD = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			S11T.SF_MIKTAR = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(sSatirNo)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub Aktar(sSatirNo)
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	satirNo = S11T.AddRow

	S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	S11T.KOD = MSYM.KOD(sSatirNo)
	S11T.LOTNUMBER = MSYM.LOTNUMBER(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SERINO = MSYM.SERINO(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R1(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R2(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R3(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_NUM1(sSatirNo)

	S11T.SF_MIKTAR = MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(sSatirNo)
	S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(sSatirNo)
End Sub
Sub Kaydet()
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")

	S11T.Empty
	For i = 1 to MSYM.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S11T.AddRow
		S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S11T.KOD = MSYM.KOD(i)
		S11T.LOTNUMBER = MSYM.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S11T.SERINO = MSYM.SERINO(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MSYM.OPERASYON(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

		S11T.SF_MIKTAR = MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(i)
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : AK2010 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : e
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call KaynakDagitimHesapla()
	if trim(MMPS10E.STATUS) <> "R" and trim(MMPS10E.FIXEDRECETE) <> "E" Then
		msgbox BaglantiliMPSKontrolHata
	End if
End Sub
Sub KaynakDagitimHesapla()
	For i = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
		if MMPS10TM.KAYNAKDAGITIMANAHTAR(i) = 0 Then
			MMPS10TM.KAYNAKDAGITIMANAHTAR(i) = 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub BaglantiliMPSleriDegistir()
	Set ss = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			BaglantiliMPS = GetBaglantiliMPS(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), MMPS10T.TRNUM(i))
			if BaglantiliMPS(0) <> "" Then
				if not Doc1Yaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
					Set M10TM = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
					Doc1.Load_Voucher BaglantiliMPS(0)
					Degisti = False
					For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
						if M10TM.TRNUM(j) = BaglantiliMPS(1) Then
							M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j) = MMPS10T.TRNUM(i)
							Degisti = True
							Exit For
						End if
					Next
					if Degisti Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetBaglantiliMPS(MPSNo, YariMamulKodu, MPSTRNUM)
	Dim BaglantiArr(1) 
	QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP'&KAYNAKMPS='" & MPSNo & "'&MAMULSTOKKODU='" & YariMamulKodu & _
				"'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM<>'" & MPSTRNUM & "'"
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dd4gy7jjj7", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		BaglantiArr(0) = M10TM.EVRAKNO
		BaglantiArr(1) = M10TM.TRNUM
	End if
	GetBaglantiliMPS = BaglantiArr
End Function
